- Synthesis: Unpaid service and a stipulated wage define two labor regimes. Against compulsion stand easy giving, spirited generosity, undertaking, stewardship, gradual cultivation, and compensation for completed work.

#### Subchannel E. Marriage, courtship, and household transition
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A woman moves between youth, courtship, marriage, separation, and renewed social attachment.
- Active motifs: الجارية من النساء وصباها (`quranic:root_000240:B004/m01`); الاستئناس والانبساط (`quranic:root_000563:B007/m01`); المرأة المراسل (`quranic:root_000563:B009/m01`); الإملاك والتزويج (`quranic:root_001444:B004/m01`); أهل البيت من بني الأعمام (`quranic:root_000537:B007/m01`); ترية الحيض (`quranic:root_000531:B007/m01`); إلقاء الثقل في المخالطة (`quranic:root_000482:B009/m01`); المواقعة في الجماع (`quranic:root_001675:B014/m01`)

- Synthesis: Young woman, social familiarity, courted widow or divorcee, marriage contract, cousins, menstrual sign, sexual weight, and intercourse describe household formation through bodily and legal transitions. The scene keeps gendered life distinct from the pastoral breeding channel even where operations resonate.

### 15. P1: The body fails, suppurates, and repairs
- Semantic invariant: Injury is tracked from bodily disturbance through wound, infection, breakage, weakness, and attempted repair.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:7-8's corpses and 69:16's weakened sky press cosmic destruction into bodily diagnostics.
- Surprising reach: The lexical field reads ruin through lung, eye, hand, bone, belly, skin, wound-fluid, and the motion of a fevered or mentally disturbed body.

#### Subchannel A. Wound, swelling, and gathered discharge
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An injury closes around fluid, swells, and becomes visibly corrupt.
- Active motifs: الجائية من الجراح (`quranic:root_000281:B006/m01`); جائية الجراح (`quranic:root_000282:B003/m01`); امتلاء الجرح بالمدة (`quranic:root_001664:B004/m01`); فساد القرحة بالمدة (`quranic:root_000025:B011/m01`); حال تأخذ في الجسم (`quranic:root_000018:B007/m01`); خواء الجوف (`quranic:root_000450:B005/m01`); إيهاء اليد بكسر أو إصابة (`quranic:root_001688:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ج ي ء`: 69:9 (جَآءَ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `و ه ي`: 69:16 (وَاهِيَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Wound-fluid gathered in an abscess, pus contained or released, a corrupted sore, illness taking the body, an emptied abdomen, and a struck or broken hand describe the progression from injury to infected bodily cavity.

#### Subchannel B. Bone, socket, side, and crooked repair
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Hard parts fracture or separate and are brought back into relation, sometimes with residual misalignment.
- Active motifs: حق يطابق موضعه كالمفصل والوعاء (`quranic:root_000347:B011/m01`); التحام العظم بعد الكسر (`quranic:root_001664:B003/m01`); عرشا العنق وظهر القدم (`quranic:root_001000:B007/m01`); مواضع علو أو شق في الجسد والآلة (`quranic:root_001188:B013/m01`); نتوء أصل الفخذ (`quranic:root_000537:B006/m01`); النصف والشق المقابل (`quranic:root_000807:B002/m01`); غلظ الخلقة والجسم (`quranic:root_000217:B003/m01`); راحة الكف (`quranic:root_000609:B014/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ)
- Synthesis: Joint-socket, knitted bone, sides of neck and foot, upper bodily junction, groin prominence, painful half, thick skeleton, and palm of the hand map an articulated body. Repair restores gathered strength even when the healed line remains crooked.

#### Subchannel C. Fever, madness, impaired sight, and disordered motion
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Disease or mental disturbance alters perception, temperature, and controlled movement.
- Active motifs: رقية تمسك وتحبس (`quranic:root_000018:B004/m01`); حال تأخذ في الجسم (`quranic:root_000018:B007/m01`); الأَرْض الزكام (`quranic:root_000025:B009/m01`); المأروض المخبول من أهل الأرض (`quranic:root_000025:B012/m01`); قوام في قوائم الشاة (`quranic:root_001273:B020/m01`); عين قائمة ذاهبة البصر (`quranic:root_001273:B021/m01`); رئي من الجن (`quranic:root_000531:B008/m01`); الرئة وما يصيبها (`quranic:root_000531:B009/m01`); تخوية النفساء (`quranic:root_000450:B006/m01`); وَهِيَ إذا حمق (`quranic:root_001688:B005/m01`); تصعد النفس وانتفاخه (`quranic:root_000537:B004/m01`); وجع قائم بالعضو (`quranic:root_001273:B019/m01`); كثرة ماء القلب على جهة البلادة (`quranic:root_001458:B008/m01`); نفخ بها بمعنى حبق (`quranic:root_001529:B004/m01`); كية الوقاع (`quranic:root_001675:B015/m01`)

#### Subchannel D. Hunger, wasted effort, and desperate appetite
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Lack of nourishment weakens the body while need drives it toward frantic competition.
- Active motifs: خواء الجوف (`quranic:root_000450:B005/m01`); انكسار الحاجة والصعلكة (`quranic:root_001596:B004/m01`); استهلاك الجهد واستفراغه (`quranic:root_001596:B009/m01`); شرَه النفس وتهافتها (`quranic:root_001596:B012/m01`); قوام وعماد ومعاش (`quranic:root_001273:B009/m01`); حبس بعض الشيء وادخاره (`quranic:root_000142:B004/m01`); الراحة والاستراحة والإراحة (`quranic:root_000609:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ب ق ي`: 69:8 (بَاقِيَةٍ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ)
- Synthesis: Empty interior produces weakness; needy dependents seek support, effort consumes itself, and appetite turns into competitive rush. Sustenance is the body's mainstay, saved reserve delays exhaustion, and rest marks temporary recovery.

### 16. P1: Fabrication joins containers, cloth, and wood
- Semantic invariant: Making consists of containing, joining, cutting, fitting, and forming materials for practical use.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:11's vessel, 69:13's horn, 69:16's torn sky, and 69:17's throne activate a dense material-craft layer.
- Surprising reach: The same operations make a bag, quiver, garment, trellis, well frame, polished stick, image, and repaired bone.

#### Subchannel A. Container, closure, and gathered contents
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A fabricated boundary gathers loose contents and controls their release.
- Active motifs: وعاء وجمع في مقرع (`quranic:root_001219:B012/m01`); ضم الشيء في وعاء (`quranic:root_001664:B002/m01`); ربابة تجمع القداح (`quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`); حق يطابق موضعه كالمفصل والوعاء (`quranic:root_000347:B011/m01`); خرقة إنزال القدر (`quranic:root_000248:B007/m01`); صماغا الفم (`quranic:root_000891:B010/m01`); قبول الشيء برضا (`quranic:root_001198:B004/m01`); موضع يمسك الماء (`quranic:root_000018:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً)
- Synthesis: Bag, generic vessel, lot-container, fitted box or socket, heat cloth, mouth corners, udder or fruit cover, and water basin all depend on enclosure. Each controls a different content, but the operation remains gathering within a bounded, accessible form.

#### Subchannel B. Weaving, joining, and crosswise reinforcement
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Flexible or segmented material is joined into a surface that can bear use.
- Active motifs: إحكام رصين في نسج أو كلام (`quranic:root_000347:B010/m01`); إحكام النسج (`quranic:root_000217:B007/m01`); أجزاء موصولة يقابل بعضها بعضا (`quranic:root_001198:B010/m01`); قطعة منشقة وثوب (`quranic:root_000807:B006/m01`); الشيء الموضوع عرضا أو المعترض عرضيا (`quranic:root_001001:B013/m01`); ما يستر أو يستتر (`quranic:root_000428:B002/m01`); الإراض البساط الضخم (`quranic:root_000025:B005/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ); `خ ف ي`: 69:18 (تَخْفَىٰ/خَافِيَةٌ); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ)
- Synthesis: Tight weave, joined straps, a cut piece of cloth, crossbeam, covering layer, heavy rug, and transport fittings move from thread-scale joining to load-bearing textile. Their quality is measured by firmness, fit, and whether the surface continues to cover.

#### Subchannel C. Wood, cutting edge, and shaped support
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Wood is selected, stripped, cut, polished, and raised as tool or frame.
- Active motifs: عود من خشب وطيب وآلة (`quranic:root_001058:B010/m01`); قراع صلب ممسوح (`quranic:root_001219:B011/m01`); غصن ساقط أو يابس يتخذ منه (`quranic:root_000859:B007/m01`); السقف والمظلّة المرفوعة (`quranic:root_001000:B002/m01`); تعريش الكرم والعنب (`quranic:root_001000:B003/m01`); خشب البئر وموضع الساقي (`quranic:root_001000:B004/m01`); قطع حاسم (`quranic:root_000322:B001/m01`); الدق والهدم حتى التسوية (`quranic:root_000482:B001/m01`); تصفية الدقيق بالمنخل (`quranic:root_001483:B002/m01`); حد الحديد وبرى الحجر (`quranic:root_001675:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ص ر ع`: 69:7 (صَرْعَىٰ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ح س م`: 69:7 (حُسُومًا); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `ن خ ل`: 69:7 (نَخْلٍ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)
- Synthesis: Stick, hard stripped shield or rod, fallen dry branch, roof timber, vine trellis, well frame, decisive cutting, pounding, sieving, and sharpening stone form a wood-and-edge working scene. Destruction and construction use many of the same operations but reverse their purpose.

#### Subchannel D. Form, silhouette, and represented body
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A thing is apprehended or reproduced by outline, proportion, and recognizable configuration.
- Active motifs: الميل والإمالة إلى جهة (`quranic:root_000891:B001/m01`); الصورة والهيئة المصورة (`quranic:root_000891:B003/m01`); جماعة النخل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000891:B005/m01`); قطيع البقر (`quranic:root_000891:B006/m01`); حكة الرأس (`quranic:root_000891:B008/m01`); صماغا الفم (`quranic:root_000891:B010/m01`); مرأى ومنظر ومرآة (`quranic:root_000531:B006/m01`); راية منصوبة (`quranic:root_000531:B011/m01`); قامة وقوام الجسم والطول (`quranic:root_001273:B011/m01`); آلة قائمة وجزء قائم (`quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`); غلظ الخلقة والجسم (`quranic:root_000217:B003/m01`); خلقة مطبوعة (`quranic:root_000217:B004/m01`); العرض خلاف الطول والجانب (`quranic:root_001001:B001/m01`); إحداث الشيء وصنعه (`quranic:root_000248:B001/m01`); تصيير الشيء على حال (`quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`); الشروع في الفعل أو ملازمته (`quranic:root_000248:B004/m01`); قصر مع سمن ولجاج (`quranic:root_000248:B012/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ)
- Synthesis: Tilted body, created image, palm cluster, cattle herd, scalp-mark, mouth corners, mirror-view, raised standard, stature, standing component, thick constitution, short stout stubborn profile, disposition, breadth, making, transformation, and entry into sustained action all encode form. The scene moves between natural body, manufactured image, and the process that gives either a recognizable state.

### 17. P2: The record passes from custody to public reading
- Semantic invariant: Judgment is staged as a transfer chain: a record arrives, is placed in a hand, opened to speech, received by an audience, and understood as one's own account.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:19-20 and 69:25-26 repeat the giving of the book, the right or left hand, the call to read, and knowledge or ignorance of the account.
- Surprising reach: The book is simultaneously joined material, written text, binding decree, official register, and an emancipation contract whose terms determine freedom.

#### Subchannel A. Delivery, taking, and displayed possession
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: An authorized object is brought, given, taken into the hand, and shown to others.
- Active motifs: الإتيان والمجيء (`quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`); الإيتاء والإعطاء (`quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`); مأتى الأمر وتهيؤه (`quranic:root_000009:B003/m01`); نَفاذ الرجل (`quranic:root_000009:B013/m01`); ها للمناولة والأخذ (`quranic:root_001573:B001/m01`); ها للتنبيه والافتتاح (`quranic:root_001573:B002/m01`); حوز الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`); توافي شيئين متقابلين (`quranic:root_001372:B004/m01`); تلقي الكلام أخذا وتلقينا (`quranic:root_001372:B011/m01`); نظم الحروف واسم المكتوب (`quranic:root_001283:B002/m01`); إخراج القول بالنطق (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ه ا ء`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ء خ ذ`: 69:30 (خُذُ); `ل ق ي`: 69:20 (مُلَٰقٍ); `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ)
- Synthesis: Arrival and giving culminate in the deictic act 'take': an object becomes present, transferable, and possessed. Encounter and verbal reception turn physical delivery into learned content; written record and spoken statement make the possession publicly legible. The `ه ا ء` motifs are branch-attested but have no available surface-root anchor.

#### Subchannel B. Joined sheet, written text, and gathered recitation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Separate marks and materials are joined into a text, then gathered again in ordered vocal reading.
- Active motifs: ضم شيء إلى شيء (`quranic:root_001283:B001/m01`); نظم الحروف واسم المكتوب (`quranic:root_001283:B002/m01`); جمع واجتماع (`quranic:root_001210:B001/m01`); قراءة وتلاوة وإقراء (`quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`); قارئ ناسك متفقه (`quranic:root_001210:B006/m01`); طريقة ومثال وقصد (`quranic:root_001210:B011/m01`); جمع اللفظ وقراءته (`quranic:root_001211:B001/m01`); مثال الشعر وطريقته (`quranic:root_001211:B005/m01`); إبلاغ السلام بصيغته (`quranic:root_001211:B006/m01`); اللسان آلة القول (`quranic:root_001272:B002/m01`); كثرة القول في صاحبه (`quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`); القول الفاشي بين الناس (`quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`); المقاولة في الأمر (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ)
- Synthesis: Joining leather and assembling cavalry supply the material root of the book; ordered letters produce the written object. Reading gathers its units into recitation, study, and a practiced way, while tongue, fluent speaker, circulating report, and negotiation specify the social performance through which the text is heard.

#### Subchannel C. Account, inquiry, and anticipated encounter
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person estimates the coming reckoning, seeks information, and finally meets the measured outcome.
- Active motifs: العد والحساب (`quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`); الحسبان والظن (`quranic:root_000318:B002/m01`); الحسبة والنظر في الأمر (`quranic:root_000318:B006/m01`); التحسب والاستخبار (`quranic:root_000318:B010/m01`); يقين بعد أمارة (`quranic:root_000969:B001/m01`); شك وتوهم (`quranic:root_000969:B003/m01`); أمر لا يوثق بعاقبته (`quranic:root_000969:B006/m01`); الدراية والعلم (`quranic:root_000473:B001/m01`); توافي شيئين متقابلين (`quranic:root_001372:B004/m01`); ما يلقاه المرء من خير أو شر (`quranic:root_001372:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن); `د ر ي`: 69:26 (أَدْرِ); `ل ق ي`: 69:20 (مُلَٰقٍ)
- Synthesis: Counting, estimation, practical review, and inquiry constitute the account. Strong conviction and weak conjecture are separated by whether the outcome can be trusted; knowledge names that epistemic threshold, and encounter turns prediction into the actual good or harm one receives.

#### Subchannel D. Right-hand allocation and left-hand deprivation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The side of reception allocates blessing, force, or loss before the record is read.
- Active motifs: اليمن والبركة (`quranic:root_001698:B001/m01`); اليد اليمنى والجهة اليمنى (`quranic:root_001698:B002/m01`); يمين القوة والحق (`quranic:root_001698:B004/m01`); التيمن الموت (`quranic:root_001698:B007/m01`); إحاطة واشتمال من الجوانب (`quranic:root_000819:B001/m01`); جهة الشمال خلاف اليمين (`quranic:root_000819:B006/m01`); الشمال موضع شؤم أو خساسة (`quranic:root_000819:B013/m01`); الإيتاء والإعطاء (`quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`); إثبات يوجب حكما أو قدرا (`quranic:root_001283:B003/m01`); إدخال الاسم في سجل أو زمرة (`quranic:root_001283:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ي م ن`: 69:19 (يَمِينِ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ)
- Synthesis: The right side carries blessing and effective power but also the mortal posture of being laid on one's right. The left encloses from the opposite side and bears a lexical association with failed fortune; the record itself is a binding decree and official enrollment, so handedness visibly assigns the recipient's place within it.

### 18. P2: Accounting becomes settlement of rights and wealth
- Semantic invariant: Calculation issues in a binding settlement involving debt, obligation, property, authority, and the sufficiency or failure of accumulated means.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:20 and 69:26 name the account, while 69:28-29 explicitly deny the utility of wealth and announce the loss of authority.
- Surprising reach: The failed wealth scene reaches loan contracts, tribute, appraised reputation, marriage as sufficiency, land revenue, and a slave's staged payments toward release.

#### Subchannel A. Decree, registration, debt, and completed payment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A right is entered, adjudicated, and discharged through an enforceable record.
- Active motifs: إثبات يوجب حكما أو قدرا (`quranic:root_001283:B003/m01`); إدخال الاسم في سجل أو زمرة (`quranic:root_001283:B004/m01`); مكاتبة العبد على عتقه (`quranic:root_001283:B005/m01`); العد والحساب (`quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`); الحسبة والنظر في الأمر (`quranic:root_000318:B006/m01`); الحكم والأمر القاطع (`quranic:root_001237:B001/m01`); الإبلاغ والوصية القاطعة (`quranic:root_001237:B002/m01`); إحكام الصنع وإنفاذ الفعل (`quranic:root_001237:B005/m01`); أداء الحق واستيفاؤه (`quranic:root_001237:B006/m01`); المال المقدم (`quranic:root_000733:B005/m01`); الإتاوة المؤداة (`quranic:root_000009:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ)
- Synthesis: The document fixes judgment, enrolls a name, or schedules emancipation payments. Counting and public oversight support adjudication; notification, crafted decree, debt settlement, an advance, and tribute complete the legal-economic circuit from entry to discharge.

#### Subchannel B. Capital, income, and the limit of sufficiency
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Property is acquired and produces income, yet is tested by whether it actually suffices or substitutes for the person.
- Active motifs: اتخاذ المال وكثرته (`quranic:root_001457:B001/m01`); الغنى والاستغناء (`quranic:root_001110:B001/m01`); الغَناء والكفاية (`quranic:root_001110:B002/m01`); الغنى بالمكان (`quranic:root_001110:B004/m01`); الكفاية والإغناء (`quranic:root_000318:B003/m01`); الحسب والمآثر (`quranic:root_000318:B004/m01`); دخل يخرج من أصل ثابت (`quranic:root_001102:B011/m01`); استهلاك المال وأخذه (`quranic:root_000043:B004/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `م و ل`: 69:28 (مَالِ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ)
- Synthesis: Accumulated wealth, independence, adequacy, settled estates, inherited reputation, and property income define resources that appear durable. Consumption or seizure of others' property and provision as a source of livelihood test the moral quality of that wealth; the decisive question is whether any of it can stand in for the owner.

#### Subchannel C. Authority, proof, and sharp public power
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Rule depends on coercive capacity, a prevailing proof, delegated office, and a tongue able to impose its claim.
- Active motifs: السلاطة قوة قاهرة مسلطة (`quranic:root_000732:B001/m01`); السلطان حجة قاهرة (`quranic:root_000732:B002/m01`); السلطان قدرة وولاية (`quranic:root_000732:B003/m01`); لسان سليط حاد صخاب (`quranic:root_000732:B004/m01`); حدة وطول في الآلة والعضو (`quranic:root_000732:B006/m01`); القيل صاحب القول النافذ (`quranic:root_001272:B004/m01`); اقتالة الحكم على غيره (`quranic:root_001272:B010/m01`); الحكم والأمر القاطع (`quranic:root_001237:B001/m01`); الحسب والمآثر (`quranic:root_000318:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ل ط`: 69:29 (سُلْطَٰنِ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ)
- Synthesis: Coercion, demonstrative proof, delegated rule, sharp speech, and hard elongated instruments materialize 'authority.' The authoritative speaker and imposed decision show power becoming discourse, while inherited standing reveals the social capital that vanishes with the condemned person's dominion.

#### Subchannel D. Marriage, alliance, and economic self-sufficiency
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Households join through contract and mutual relation, promising social and material sufficiency.
- Active motifs: مكاتبة العبد على عتقه (`quranic:root_001283:B005/m01`); أداء الحق واستيفاؤه (`quranic:root_001237:B006/m01`); سلف المصاهرة (`quranic:root_000733:B006/m01`); الغانية المستغنية (`quranic:root_001110:B005/m01`); الغنى والتزويج (`quranic:root_001110:B006/m01`); ائتلاف الشمل بعد تفرق (`quranic:root_000819:B002/m01`); المراضاة والتراضي رضا متبادل (`quranic:root_000569:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ)
- Synthesis: Emancipation contract and paid right provide a legal template; intermarried in-laws, the self-sufficient married woman, marriage as protection from need, reunited household, and mutual satisfaction provide the social result. Settlement is therefore also the making or unmaking of durable relations.

### 19. P2: The garden lowers its harvest to the eater
- Semantic invariant: Cultivation joins sheltering growth, vertical abundance, ripened fruit, timely cutting, and bodily access.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:22-24 locates the rewarded person in a high garden with low-hanging clusters and commands eating and drinking.
- Surprising reach: Elevation is reversed into service: the high place does not distance its produce but bends it close, while past action appears as seed, advance, and first-flowing yield.

#### Subchannel A. Sheltering orchard and dense growth
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Interlaced vegetation creates a covered garden whose density both conceals and sustains.
- Active motifs: الستر والاستتار (`quranic:root_000266:B001/m01`); غشيان الليل (`quranic:root_000266:B002/m01`); البستان المستور بالشجر (`quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`); التفاف النبات واندفاعه (`quranic:root_000266:B011/m01`); المَجَنَّة موضع الاستتار (`quranic:root_000266:B017/m01`); أرض ونبت ريان (`quranic:root_000783:B014/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); غلة الشجر والزرع (`quranic:root_000043:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ)
- Synthesis: Cover, night-like enclosure, tree garden, dense growth, and a place of shelter make the orchard an inhabited canopy. Moist wooded ground, ripening fruit, and crop yield supply its productive interior, so concealment is life-supporting rather than evasive.

#### Subchannel B. Ripening, cutting, and gathered cluster
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Fruit reaches its time, is cut from its stem, and remains as an accessible cluster or usable remainder.
- Active motifs: أخذ الشيء بقطعه من أصله (`quranic:root_001241:B001/m01`); الثمر المقطوف والعنقود الداني (`quranic:root_001241:B002/m01`); دنو القِطاف وحلول وقته (`quranic:root_001241:B003/m01`); ما يسقط من القِطاف (`quranic:root_001241:B004/m01`); نبات رخص عريض الورق (`quranic:root_001241:B008/m01`); غلة الشجر والزرع (`quranic:root_000043:B002/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); إطعام الغصن وقبول الوصل (`quranic:root_000934:B010/m01`); خروج النماء والنتاج (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); السلافة والخلاصة الأولى (`quranic:root_000733:B002/m01`); أكل القضى من عجم الزبيب (`quranic:root_001237:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ)
- Synthesis: Cut fruit, the cluster itself, harvest time, dropped gleanings, edible leafy plant, crop yield, ripened produce, accepted graft, emerging return, first pressed liquid, and eaten raisin pits trace a harvest from plant to gathered food and residue. Cutting here realizes maturity rather than destruction.

#### Subchannel C. Height that bends into nearness
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An elevated setting places its usable upper growth within reach of the eater.
- Active motifs: السمو والارتفاع (`quranic:root_001042:B001/m01`); الرفعة والشرف (`quranic:root_001042:B002/m01`); الجهة العليا ومن فوق (`quranic:root_001042:B005/m01`); الشيء المحمول على الأعلى (`quranic:root_001042:B008/m01`); الطول والضخامة (`quranic:root_001042:B010/m01`); القرب والمقاربة (`quranic:root_000493:B001/m01`); الدنيا والأدنى (`quranic:root_000493:B002/m01`); دنو الأعلى من الوسط (`quranic:root_000493:B005/m01`); الثمر المقطوف والعنقود الداني (`quranic:root_001241:B002/m01`); مشي الدابة المتقارب البطيء (`quranic:root_001241:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع ل و`: 69:22 (عَالِيَةٍ); `د ن و`: 69:23 (دَانِيَةٌ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ)
- Synthesis: Height, honor, upper side, top-load, and tall bodily form establish vertical abundance. Nearness, the lower world as what is close, and a bent upper body reverse distance; the slow close-stepping animal gives the same geometry motion, and the low cluster becomes the point where height meets hand.

#### Subchannel D. Past advance becomes present yield
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Earlier action is stored like seed, capital, or first provision and returns as consumable produce.
- Active motifs: التقدم والسبق (`quranic:root_000733:B001/m01`); السلفة قبل الطعام (`quranic:root_000733:B003/m01`); المال المقدم (`quranic:root_000733:B005/m01`); الأرض المسلوفة (`quranic:root_000733:B010/m01`); خروج النماء والنتاج (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); غلة الشجر والزرع (`quranic:root_000043:B002/m01`); النصيب والرزق المطعوم (`quranic:root_000043:B003/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); مضي الزمن وذهاب القرن (`quranic:root_000436:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ)
- Synthesis: What went before includes prior deed, advance meal, invested capital, and leveled seedbed. Its return appears as crop, allotted food, livelihood, and ripeness; elapsed generations give the causal sequence temporal depth. Reward is thus materialized as a yield whose conditions were laid down earlier.

### 20. P2: Hospitality joins appetite, vessel, and ease
- Semantic invariant: A complete meal requires food and drink, allotted shares, serving vessels, bodily reception, and freedom from harmful aftermath.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:24 commands eating and drinking in ease, while 69:34-37 inverts hospitality into withheld or corrupt food.
- Surprising reach: The table extends into pre-meal provision, a drinker's cohort, a sealed waterskin, congrats at good fortune, pasture for animals, and the social economy that sustains guests.

#### Subchannel A. Meal, drink, portion, and shared company
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Food and liquid are taken in assigned shares by an eating and drinking company.
- Active motifs: تناول المطعوم (`quranic:root_000043:B001/m01`); النصيب والرزق المطعوم (`quranic:root_000043:B003/m01`); وعاء الأكل وموضعه (`quranic:root_000043:B013/m01`); تناول المائع (`quranic:root_000783:B001/m01`); نصيب الماء (`quranic:root_000783:B002/m01`); جماعة الشرب والمشارب (`quranic:root_000783:B003/m01`); موضع الشرب وآلته (`quranic:root_000783:B004/m01`); ذوق الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000934:B001/m01`); إطعام الغير وطلب الطعام (`quranic:root_000934:B002/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`); السلفة قبل الطعام (`quranic:root_000733:B003/m01`); هناءة بلا مشقة ولا وخامة (`quranic:root_001604:B001/m01`); الهنء عطاء وكفاية (`quranic:root_001604:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا)
- Synthesis: Eating, allotted livelihood, bowl, drinking, water-share, drinking group, room or vessel, taste, feeding, livelihood, first course, effortless digestion, and ample gift form the full hospitality frame. The meal is social because shares, servers, and companions are built into its lexical structure.

#### Subchannel B. Vessel preparation and controlled pouring
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Containers are lined, sealed, seasoned, and fitted so food or liquid can be served without loss.
- Active motifs: موضع الشرب وآلته (`quranic:root_000783:B004/m01`); تطييب القربة وتشريبها (`quranic:root_000783:B013/m01`); وعاء الأكل وموضعه (`quranic:root_000043:B013/m01`); السلف الجراب (`quranic:root_000733:B008/m01`); السلفة بطانة الخف (`quranic:root_000733:B013/m01`); خرقة تحبس صب الماء (`quranic:root_001102:B008/m01`); نوى مخلوط بالعلف (`quranic:root_001102:B010/m01`); موضع الاغتسال وآلة التسخين (`quranic:root_000001:B013/m01`); السلاسة في الجري والشرب (`quranic:root_000731:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `س ل س ل`: 69:32 (سِلْسِلَةٍ)
- Synthesis: Drinking chamber or cup, seasoned waterskin, eating vessel, large bag, shoe-lining as a thin inner layer, spout filter, feed mixed with date stones, bath vessel, and smooth-flowing drink specify preparation at the container boundary. Taste and flow depend on what lines, seals, filters, and holds the contents.

#### Subchannel C. Ease, approval, and congratulated well-being
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A beneficial provision is accepted without aversion and acknowledged as a good state.
- Active motifs: هناءة بلا مشقة ولا وخامة (`quranic:root_001604:B001/m01`); الهنء عطاء وكفاية (`quranic:root_001604:B002/m01`); هناءة الماشية من النبت (`quranic:root_001604:B003/m01`); تهنئة بخير وولاية (`quranic:root_001604:B005/m01`); الرضا خلاف السخط (`quranic:root_000569:B001/m01`); الرضوان والمرضاة اسم للرضا الكثير أو المطلوب (`quranic:root_000569:B002/m01`); المراضاة والتراضي رضا متبادل (`quranic:root_000569:B003/m01`); الإرضاء طلب رضا الغير وإزالة سخطه (`quranic:root_000569:B004/m01`); الرِّضا خلاف السخط والقبول (`quranic:root_000570:B001/m01`); الكفاية والإغناء (`quranic:root_000318:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ); `ر ض ي`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ)
- Synthesis: Wholesome ease, adequate gift, pasture share, congratulation, acceptance, abundant satisfaction, mutual approval, active pleasing, and sufficiency describe well-being as both bodily and relational. The `ر ض ي` acceptance motif is lexically available but has no surface-root anchor.

#### Subchannel D. Animal care at the edge of the feast
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Pasture, drink, coating, and fatness maintain the animals whose products support human provision.
- Active motifs: هناءة الماشية من النبت (`quranic:root_001604:B003/m01`); الهِناء قطران يطلى به البعير (`quranic:root_001604:B004/m01`); سمن الحيوان وطعم الشحم (`quranic:root_000934:B007/m01`); مستطعم الفرس وطلب جريه (`quranic:root_000934:B009/m01`); نصيب الماء (`quranic:root_000783:B002/m01`); أرض ونبت ريان (`quranic:root_000783:B014/m01`); كرائم المال والإبل (`quranic:root_000001:B014/m01`); السبع الحيوان المفترس (`quranic:root_000669:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `س ب ع`: 69:32 (سَبْعُونَ)
- Synthesis: Animals receive plant share, protective tar, body fat, induced running, allotted water, lush ground, and the status of choice stock; predation defines the risk at the boundary. Hospitality rests on an attended pastoral resource, not food appearing without material support.

### 21. P2: Handedness organizes enclosure and omen
- Semantic invariant: Right and left are not mere coordinates: they distribute blessing, force, enclosure, social cohesion, cold, and ominous rank.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:19 and 69:25 contrast the right and left hands as the first visible division between outcomes.
- Surprising reach: A side becomes a garment, wind, covered weapon, herd-binding device, and social 'gatheredness,' giving handed judgment a full material frame.

#### Subchannel A. Right side, oath, blessing, and effective force
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The right hand receives, pledges, and acts from a culturally favored side.
- Active motifs: اليمن والبركة (`quranic:root_001698:B001/m01`); اليد اليمنى والجهة اليمنى (`quranic:root_001698:B002/m01`); يمين الحلف (`quranic:root_001698:B003/m01`); يمين القوة والحق (`quranic:root_001698:B004/m01`); اليمن البلد والانتساب (`quranic:root_001698:B005/m01`); التيمن الموت (`quranic:root_001698:B007/m01`); الإيتاء والإعطاء (`quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`); حوز الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ي م ن`: 69:19 (يَمِينِ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:30 (خُذُ)
- Synthesis: Blessing, right hand, oath, force, geographic rightwardness, and mortal right-side posture gather around receiving and taking. Giving and possession complete the hand-to-hand transfer; the favored side is simultaneously bodily, juridical, and agentive.

#### Subchannel B. Left side, cold current, and failed lot
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The opposite hand marks spatial contrast and carries associations of cold exposure and unfavorable assignment.
- Active motifs: جهة الشمال خلاف اليمين (`quranic:root_000819:B006/m01`); ريح الشمال وما تبرده (`quranic:root_000819:B007/m01`); الشمول خمرا باردة أو مغطية للعقل (`quranic:root_000819:B009/m01`); الشمال موضع شؤم أو خساسة (`quranic:root_000819:B013/m01`); اليد اليمنى والجهة اليمنى (`quranic:root_001698:B002/m01`); حمحمة الفرس (`quranic:root_000001:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ي م ن`: 69:19 (يَمِينِ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ)
- Synthesis: Left hand and north wind establish side and temperature; cold wine overlays the mind, ominous bird or left-hand lot marks failed fortune, and an animal's low vocalization supplies the tense soundscape. The contrast is not a claim that every left sense is punitive, but a local convergence around adverse allocation.

#### Subchannel C. Wrapping, encompassing, and concealed load
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A side-cloth closes around body or object and may hide what is being carried.
- Active motifs: إحاطة واشتمال من الجوانب (`quranic:root_000819:B001/m01`); لف الثوب واشتماله (`quranic:root_000819:B003/m01`); شد وعاء يشتمل على الحيوان أو النخلة (`quranic:root_000819:B004/m01`); حمل مستور في الاشتمال (`quranic:root_000819:B005/m01`); الستر والاستتار (`quranic:root_000266:B001/m01`); الجُنّة الواقية (`quranic:root_000266:B008/m01`); غلالة بين الثياب (`quranic:root_001102:B007/m01`); القطيفة ذات الخمل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_001241:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ)
- Synthesis: Encompassing, cloak, tied udder or fruit cover, hidden short sword or stolen animal, generic concealment, shield, undergarment, and plush covering make sidedness into an enclosing textile operation. What the hand receives may be displayed, but what the garment holds can remain unseen.

#### Subchannel D. Reunited group and inherited disposition
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Scattered persons are gathered into one social whole with recognizable habits and rank.
- Active motifs: ائتلاف الشمل بعد تفرق (`quranic:root_000819:B002/m01`); شمائل الإنسان وخلائقه (`quranic:root_000819:B008/m01`); الشمال موضع شؤم أو خساسة (`quranic:root_000819:B013/m01`); سواد الناس وجماعتهم (`quranic:root_000266:B013/m01`); المراضاة والتراضي رضا متبادل (`quranic:root_000569:B003/m01`); الرضي صفة للمطيع أو المحب أو الضامن (`quranic:root_000569:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ)
- Synthesis: Reunited company, personal dispositions, unfavorable status, mass of people, mutual approval, and the obedient or guaranteeing associate describe the social 'side' to which one belongs. Cohesion is ethical as well as numerical.

### 22. P2: Seizure becomes linked confinement
- Semantic invariant: Punitive control proceeds from taking the body to fixing a collar, joining links, threading the prisoner through, and measuring the completed restraint.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:30-32 orders seizure, fettering, burning, and insertion into a seventy-cubit chain.
- Surprising reach: The mechanism reaches wrestling grip, amulet-like arrest, bridle, sewing thread, linked lightning, arm-span, chokehold, and the amount of force a body can bear.

#### Subchannel A. Taking, arrest, and bodily grip
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Agents seize the condemned person by an available hold and convert mobility into custody.
- Active motifs: حوز الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`); المؤاخذة بالذنب (`quranic:root_000018:B002/m01`); القبض والأسر (`quranic:root_000018:B003/m01`); رقية تمسك وتحبس (`quranic:root_000018:B004/m01`); أخذة المصارعة (`quranic:root_000018:B011/m01`); مقبض الشيء المأخوذ به (`quranic:root_000018:B012/m01`); الذراع جارحة ممدودة (`quranic:root_000512:B001/m01`); الأخذ بالذراع وتذريعه (`quranic:root_000512:B007/m01`); فعل سبعة: المبالغة في الأخذ والشر (`quranic:root_000669:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء خ ذ`: 69:30 (خُذُ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `س ب ع`: 69:32 (سَبْعُونَ)
- Synthesis: Taking, accountability, arrest, binding charm, wrestling hold, handle, extended arm, arm-grip or choke, and exemplary harsh seizure form a capture sequence. The body is controlled first at the interface where hand meets limb.

#### Subchannel B. Collar, hidden insertion, and fixed restraint
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A device enters around or through the body and remains fixed in its interstices.
- Active motifs: دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); طوق يغل العضو (`quranic:root_001102:B006/m01`); إدخال الحبل في العنق (`quranic:root_000783:B008/m01`); الأخذ بالذراع وتذريعه (`quranic:root_000512:B007/m01`); إسكان الذبيحة بالسكين (`quranic:root_000726:B007/m01`); شد وعاء يشتمل على الحيوان أو النخلة (`quranic:root_000819:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ)
- Synthesis: Penetration between parts, iron collar, rope through neck, arm restraint, slaughtering blade that stills movement, and tied animal-cover all share the operation of fastening a living body. The punishment intensifies familiar husbandry and tool functions into immobilization.

#### Subchannel C. Threading through linked material
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A line of connected parts receives another body or material through its continuous path.
- Active motifs: اتصال الحلقات والأجزاء (`quranic:root_000731:B002/m01`); امتداد ملتف متعاقب (`quranic:root_000731:B003/m01`); تردد واضطراب متتابع (`quranic:root_000731:B004/m01`); النفاذ في الطريق (`quranic:root_000735:B001/m01`); إدخال الشيء في الشيء (`quranic:root_000735:B002/m01`); الخيط الذي يخاط به (`quranic:root_000735:B004/m01`); طرة ممتدة في ناحية الثوب (`quranic:root_000735:B005/m01`); دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); سير أو رسالة تتخلل البلاد (`quranic:root_001102:B009/m01`); ضم شيء إلى شيء (`quranic:root_001283:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ل س ل`: 69:32 (سِلْسِلَةٍ); `س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ)
- Synthesis: Iron links, a winding chain-like extension, repeated disturbance, road penetration, insertion, sewing thread, extended selvedge, deep entry, traveling message, and joined material all define serial continuity. 'Insert him' is thereby rendered as threading a body through a made line.

#### Subchannel D. Measured span, capacity, and exceeded limit
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The restraint is quantified by bodily measure and tested against the prisoner's power to endure or traverse it.
- Active motifs: القياس بالذراع (`quranic:root_000512:B002/m01`); امتداد الخطو والسعي (`quranic:root_000512:B003/m01`); الذرع طاقة ومدى (`quranic:root_000512:B004/m01`); النواحي ومشارف العمران (`quranic:root_000512:B008/m01`); على حبل الذراع حاضر (`quranic:root_000512:B009/m01`); خفيف اليد في العمل (`quranic:root_000512:B010/m01`); أعلام وأجزاء تسمى بالذراع (`quranic:root_000512:B013/m01`); العدد سبعة وما يتفرع منه (`quranic:root_000669:B001/m01`); اتصال الحلقات والأجزاء (`quranic:root_000731:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `س ب ع`: 69:32 (سَبْعُونَ); `س ل س ل`: 69:32 (سِلْسِلَةٍ)
- Synthesis: Cubit-measure, extended stride, capacity, borderlands, immediate availability, skilled hand, named arm-lengths, seventy, and linked iron place the chain in a measurable spatial world. The number exceeds ordinary bodily reach, turning measurement into a declaration of inescapability.

### 23. P2: Fire and washing are inverted into forced intake
- Semantic invariant: Heat, fuel, hot liquid, bodily discharge, and food converge in a punitive process that reverses cooking, cleansing, and nourishment.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:31 and 69:35-37 join the blaze to the absence of a friend and to corrupt food eaten only by sinners.
- Surprising reach: The scene behaves like a furnace and bath gone wrong: fire consumes fuel, heat enters the body, washing residue becomes a meal, and an implement made to straighten by flame becomes an instrument of exposure.

#### Subchannel A. Blaze, roasting, and furnace-like correction
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Fire is fed, intensified, entered, and used to roast or straighten material.
- Active motifs: تأجج النار وشدة حرها (`quranic:root_000225:B001/m01`); احتدام الحرب والموت (`quranic:root_000225:B002/m01`); تلهب الوجه بالغضب (`quranic:root_000225:B004/m01`); ملاقاة النار وحرها (`quranic:root_000880:B003/m01`); إيقاد الصلاء وتسوية الشيء بالنار (`quranic:root_000880:B004/m01`); الصلاية حجر يدق عليه (`quranic:root_000880:B009/m01`); إطعام النار واشتعالها (`quranic:root_000043:B005/m01`); السواد والفحم والدخان (`quranic:root_000001:B001/m01`); ذوبان الإهالة والشحم (`quranic:root_000001:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ج ح م`: 69:31 (جَحِيمَ); `ص ل ي`: 69:31 (صَلُّ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ)
- Synthesis: Blazing coals, battle-like heat, inflamed face, entering fire, kindling, roasting, heating a rod straight, grinding stone, fire consuming fuel, black smoke, and melted fat create a furnace process. The condemned body occupies the place of both material and fuel.

#### Subchannel B. Hot liquid, sweat, fever, and inward thirst
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Heat enters water and body, producing sweat, fever, unresolved thirst, and the nearness of death.
- Active motifs: الماء الحار والاغتسال به (`quranic:root_000001:B002/m01`); العرق وأثر الحر (`quranic:root_000001:B004/m01`); الحمى والحمام في الأبدان والأرض (`quranic:root_000001:B005/m01`); دنو الأمر وحضوره (`quranic:root_000001:B006/m01`); القضاء والقدر والموت (`quranic:root_000001:B010/m01`); حرارة تنغل في الجوف (`quranic:root_001102:B002/m01`); ماء يشرب مع كراهة (`quranic:root_000783:B005/m01`); السلاط غليل (`quranic:root_000732:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `س ل ط`: 69:29 (سُلْطَٰنِ)
- Synthesis: Scalding water, hot spring, sweat, fever, due time, decreed death, inward burning thirst, bitter drink, and raw craving track heat from environment into physiology. What would ordinarily cool and satisfy becomes another carrier of torment.

#### Subchannel C. Washing medium turned corrupt food
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Material associated with cleaning, runoff, or filtration is presented as the only available nourishment.
- Active motifs: تطهير الشيء بإسالة الماء وإزالة الدرن (`quranic:root_001087:B001/m01`); ماء الغسل وما يغسل به أو فيه (`quranic:root_001087:B002/m01`); ذوق الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000934:B001/m01`); إطعام الغير وطلب الطعام (`quranic:root_000934:B002/m01`); تناول المطعوم (`quranic:root_000043:B001/m01`); خرقة تحبس صب الماء (`quranic:root_001102:B008/m01`); موضع الاغتسال وآلة التسخين (`quranic:root_000001:B013/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `غ س ل`: 69:36 (غِسْلِينٍ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ)
- Synthesis: Washing removes grime by flowing water and uses a washing medium or place; taste, feeding, eating, spout-filter, and bath vessel then redirect those sanitation materials toward ingestion. The reversal makes the absence of proper food concrete as contamination of the food category itself.

#### Subchannel D. Consumption, corrosion, and prey
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Eating ranges from nourishment to destructive consumption of body, property, and victim.
- Active motifs: استهلاك المال وأخذه (`quranic:root_000043:B004/m01`); إطعام النار واشتعالها (`quranic:root_000043:B005/m01`); التآكل والفساد والحكة (`quranic:root_000043:B006/m01`); الفريسة والمعدة للأكل (`quranic:root_000043:B007/m01`); الإفساد والنميمة بين الناس (`quranic:root_000043:B009/m01`); الإدعاء على الغير والتمكين منه (`quranic:root_000043:B010/m01`); قلة الجماعة بقدر رأس (`quranic:root_000043:B011/m01`); قوة الشيء وتمام مادته (`quranic:root_000043:B012/m01`); طعم العقل والقيمة (`quranic:root_000934:B008/m01`); القدرة على الشيء (`quranic:root_000934:B011/m01`); الأخذ بالمطعمة عند الخنق (`quranic:root_000934:B012/m01`); السبع الحيوان المفترس (`quranic:root_000669:B002/m01`); الوقيعة والاغتياب كأذى السبع (`quranic:root_000669:B003/m01`); الخطيئة ذنب وإثم (`quranic:root_000420:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `س ب ع`: 69:32 (سَبْعُونَ); `خ ط ء`: 69:37 (خَٰطِـُٔونَ)
- Synthesis: Property consumption, devouring fire, bodily corrosion, prepared prey, social sabotage, false accusation, a group small enough for one head, material strength, reason, power, throat-grip, predator, figurative mauling, and deliberate sin expose eating as appropriation. The punitive meal makes the eater resemble the corrupting appetite that defined the offense.

#### Subchannel E. Lamp oil and controlled illumination
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Combustible material is contained and fed to a flame so heat becomes useful light.
- Active motifs: السليط زيت يضيء (`quranic:root_000732:B005/m01`); إطعام النار واشتعالها (`quranic:root_000043:B005/m01`); تأجج النار وشدة حرها (`quranic:root_000225:B001/m01`); السواد والفحم والدخان (`quranic:root_000001:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ل ط`: 69:29 (سُلْطَٰنِ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ج ح م`: 69:31 (جَحِيمَ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ)
- Synthesis: Illuminating sesame oil, fire consuming fuel, intense flame, and black smoke describe controlled combustion and its residue. This small lamp process contrasts with the unbounded blaze that consumes the condemned body.

### 24. P2: Faith is tested as trust and provision
- Semantic invariant: Belief is not isolated assent; it creates security, worshipful orientation, and active responsibility for another person's food and dwelling.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:33-34 identifies failure to believe in God and failure to urge feeding the poor as the paired causes of punishment.
- Surprising reach: Faith's lexical image is a calm heart holding a trust, while neglect of the poor is rendered as refusal to set desire, household, land, and food in motion toward them.

#### Subchannel A. Secure heart, entrusted charge, and assent
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: The heart settles into confidence, accepts a report, and proves trustworthy with what it receives.
- Active motifs: سكون القلب في أمن وثقة (`quranic:root_000054:B001/m01`); تصديق يطمئن إليه القلب (`quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`); قول آمين طلبا للاستجابة (`quranic:root_000054:B003/m01`); الرضا خلاف السخط (`quranic:root_000569:B001/m01`); تلقي الكلام أخذا وتلقينا (`quranic:root_001372:B011/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء م ن`: 69:33 (يُؤْمِنُ); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ); `ل ق ي`: 69:20 (مُلَٰقٍ)
- Synthesis: Security, trust, entrusted object, assent, and the prayer for response all turn belief into settled receptivity. Acceptance and learned reception show that faith is both relation to a report and reliability in carrying its consequences.

#### Subchannel B. Worship, greatness, and reverent scale
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A worshipper recognizes the object of devotion as weighty, powerful, and worthy of magnification.
- Active motifs: التعبد والمعبود (`quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`); اسم الله في القسم والنداء (`quranic:root_000047:B002/m01`); الكبر والقوة (`quranic:root_001029:B001/m01`); معظم الشيء (`quranic:root_001029:B002/m01`); الاستعظام والهيبة (`quranic:root_001029:B007/m01`); الحرمة والشرف (`quranic:root_001029:B010/m01`); تصديق يطمئن إليه القلب (`quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ل ه`: 69:33 (ٱللَّهِ); `ع ظ م`: 69:33 (عَظِيمِ); `ء م ن`: 69:33 (يُؤْمِنُ)
- Synthesis: Worship and the divine name orient the relation; greatness, the major portion, awe, and honored standing give it scale. Believing assent is therefore not flat acknowledgment but recognition that orders perception and conduct around what is greater.

#### Subchannel C. Prayer, blessing, and answering response
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Devotion rises as prayer and blessing and is met by a voiced response seeking acceptance.
- Active motifs: الصلاة عبادة لازمة (`quranic:root_000880:B001/m01`); الدعاء والبركة والرحمة (`quranic:root_000880:B002/m01`); الصلوات مواضع عبادة (`quranic:root_000880:B008/m01`); قول آمين طلبا للاستجابة (`quranic:root_000054:B003/m01`); ها للجواب والتلبية (`quranic:root_001573:B003/m01`); تهنئة بخير وولاية (`quranic:root_001604:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ص ل ي`: 69:31 (صَلُّ); `ء م ن`: 69:33 (يُؤْمِنُ); `ه ا ء`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا)
- Synthesis: Obligatory prayer, blessing and mercy, houses of worship, saying amen, answering a call, and congratulatory invocation form a prayer-response scene. The `ه ا ء` response motif is branch-attested but has no available surface-root anchor.

#### Subchannel D. Urging, reciprocal encouragement, and increased desire
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Speech and inward appetite are activated so that provision moves toward one who lacks it.
- Active motifs: الحَضّ على الشيء (`quranic:root_000334:B001/m01`); استزادة النفس (`quranic:root_000334:B004/m01`); إطعام الغير وطلب الطعام (`quranic:root_000934:B002/m01`); الهنء عطاء وكفاية (`quranic:root_001604:B002/m01`); العناية الصادقة بالشيء (`quranic:root_001272:B015/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ض ض`: 69:34 (يَحُضُّ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ)
- Synthesis: Exhortation can be mutual, and the self can be pressed to seek more on another's behalf. Feeding, adequate gift, and sincere care specify the intended outcome; the failure in the surface scene is a failure to initiate this social motion.

#### Subchannel E. Poor person, household, and stabilizing food
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Food and shelter arrest involuntary motion by giving a vulnerable person a place and means to remain.
- Active motifs: ذهاب الحركة (`quranic:root_000726:B001/m01`); استيطان المنزل (`quranic:root_000726:B002/m01`); أهل الدار (`quranic:root_000726:B003/m01`); مأنس السكون (`quranic:root_000726:B004/m01`); ذل المسكنة (`quranic:root_000726:B006/m01`); موضع الاستقرار (`quranic:root_000726:B009/m01`); قوت يثبت المقام (`quranic:root_000726:B010/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`); ائتلاف الشمل بعد تفرق (`quranic:root_000819:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ)
- Synthesis: Stillness, dwelling, household, familiar comfort, poverty, stable place, sustaining fodder, livelihood, and gathered company make provision a stabilizing system. The poor person is not only hungry but socially and spatially unanchored; food belongs with household continuity.

#### Subchannel F. Hidden betrayal, rancor, and shameless hostility
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A concealed moral injury remains inside a social relation until it emerges as hostility or refusal of restraint.
- Active motifs: خيانة مستترة (`quranic:root_001102:B004/m01`); ضغن كامن في الصدر (`quranic:root_001102:B005/m01`); قلة الحياء (`quranic:root_000225:B005/m01`); ظنين معاد (`quranic:root_000969:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ج ح م`: 69:31 (جَحِيمَ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن)
- Synthesis: Secret betrayal, inward rancor, lack of shame, and an enemy under suspicion form an ethical interior opposite to the secure entrusted heart. Social provision fails where hostility is stored rather than responsibility.

#### Subchannel G. Hidden beings and the unseen religious field
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Religious assent situates human conduct within a field that includes concealed nonhuman beings.
- Active motifs: الجن المستترون (`quranic:root_000266:B005/m01`); التعبد والمعبود (`quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`); تصديق يطمئن إليه القلب (`quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ء ل ه`: 69:33 (ٱللَّهِ); `ء م ن`: 69:33 (يُؤْمِنُ)
- Synthesis: Hidden spirits, worship, and believing assent form a compact unseen religious scene. The latent spirit image sharpens the difference between principled faith in a trustworthy report and uncontrolled concealment of the mind.

### 25. P2: Negation strips companion, place, and release
- Semantic invariant: The condemned state is formed by successive absences: no effective wealth, no authority, no close companion, no ordinary food, and no open path away.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:28-36 repeatedly negates benefit, authority, companion, and food.
- Surprising reach: Lexical absence expands into empty house, elapsed generation, divorce, unguarded object, stationary homebody, and grammatical exclusion.

#### Subchannel A. No companion and no ordinary category
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Negation removes available persons and things from the condemned person's present field.
- Active motifs: ليس جحود ينفي الحال كفعل جامد (`quranic:root_001390:B001/m01`); ليس استثناء يخرج المذكور (`quranic:root_001390:B002/m01`); ليس تقوم مقام لا في النسق والتبرئة (`quranic:root_001390:B003/m01`); الحميم والحامة في القربى (`quranic:root_000001:B011/m01`); إخراج المستثنى من الحكم (`quranic:root_000436:B004/m01`); خلاك ذم (`quranic:root_000436:B005/m01`); البراءة والخروج من التبعة (`quranic:root_000436:B006/m01`); ها للتنبيه والافتتاح (`quranic:root_001573:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ل ي س`: 69:35 (لَيْسَ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ); `ه ا ء`: unavailable (surface root missing)
- Synthesis: Grammatical denial, exception, and categorical exclusion close the inventory. The close kinsman is absent; blame, liability, and affiliation are removed through formulas of exclusion and disavowal. The deictic 'here' is branch-attested under `ه ا ء` but lacks a surface-root anchor.

#### Subchannel B. Empty dwelling and elapsed inhabitants
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A place remains after its occupants, generation, or expected guardian has gone.
- Active motifs: تعري الشيء وخلو المكان (`quranic:root_000436:B001/m01`); الانفراد والخلوة (`quranic:root_000436:B002/m01`); مضي الزمن وذهاب القرن (`quranic:root_000436:B003/m01`); خلو القلب من الهم (`quranic:root_000436:B007/m01`); الخلاة لمن لا حافظ له (`quranic:root_000436:B016/m01`); الغنى بالمكان (`quranic:root_001110:B004/m01`); استيطان المنزل (`quranic:root_000726:B002/m01`); أهل الدار (`quranic:root_000726:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ)
- Synthesis: Vacant place, private isolation, elapsed generation, heart without concern, unguarded person, former dwelling, house, and household define absence through habitation. The condemned 'here' is a place whose normal social occupants and protections have been removed.

#### Subchannel C. Release, divorce, and abandoned relation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A bond is opened so each party or dependent is left to a separate course.
- Active motifs: التخلية والإرسال (`quranic:root_000436:B008/m01`); الخلو من الزوج أو الولد (`quranic:root_000436:B009/m01`); الخلية من الإبل (`quranic:root_000436:B010/m01`); المخالاة في المفارقة والمصارعة (`quranic:root_000436:B014/m01`); السخرية أو المخادعة في الخلو (`quranic:root_000436:B015/m01`); ائتلاف الشمل بعد تفرق (`quranic:root_000819:B002/m01`); المراضاة والتراضي رضا متبادل (`quranic:root_000569:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ)
- Synthesis: Release, divorced or childless status, a dam separated from her young, broken truce, mutual exposure in wrestling, deception in private, scattered company, and lost mutual satisfaction describe relational unbinding. Freedom and abandonment share the same opening operation but differ in who benefits.

#### Subchannel D. Stationary courage, weakness, and tolerated burden
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person remains in place either from steadfast courage, heavy inertia, patient tolerance, or failed judgment.
- Active motifs: الأليس شجاع لا تروعه الحرب (`quranic:root_001390:B004/m01`); الأليس ملازم لا يبرح مكانه (`quranic:root_001390:B005/m01`); تلايس احتمال وتغاض حسن الخلق (`quranic:root_001390:B006/m01`); الأليس ضعيف الرأي (`quranic:root_001390:B007/m01`); الأليس ذم لمن لا يغار (`quranic:root_001390:B008/m01`); ضعف وقلة حيلة (`quranic:root_000969:B008/m01`); ذهاب الحركة (`quranic:root_000726:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ل ي س`: 69:35 (لَيْسَ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ)
- Synthesis: Fearless fighter, immovable homebody, tolerant bearer, weak counselor, dishonored complacency, little capacity, and stillness all produce non-movement. The scene distinguishes principled staying from inability or moral indifference by the cause of the same visible posture.

### 26. P2: Hidden life proceeds toward birth
- Semantic invariant: Generation moves through concealed interior, sexual readiness, conception, gestation, visible swelling, and the appointed time of emergence.
- Surface relation: indirect; the garden's hidden growth and the rewarded 'life' in 69:21-24 activate a dense reproductive process without narrating a birth.
- Surprising reach: The process connects womb, menstrual interval, animal heat, hoped-for child, near delivery, fruit ripening, grafting, and failed fertility.

#### Subchannel A. Womb, concealed fetus, and gathered bodily matter
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An interior chamber gathers blood or seed and shelters developing life.
- Active motifs: الجنين المستور في البطن (`quranic:root_000266:B007/m01`); الجنان المستور في الصدر (`quranic:root_000266:B010/m01`); الجناجن عظام الصدر (`quranic:root_000266:B016/m01`); قرء الحيض والطهر (`quranic:root_001210:B003/m01`); رحم يجمع حملا (`quranic:root_001210:B004/m01`); وقت الشيء ودورانه (`quranic:root_001211:B002/m01`); اجتماع الرحم وحمله (`quranic:root_001211:B003/m01`); لقاح جزئي أو حمل الناقة (`quranic:root_000819:B012/m01`); دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); إمساك للاستبراء أو حبس (`quranic:root_001210:B010/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ)
- Synthesis: Fetus, inward heart-chamber, chest bones, menstrual or pure interval, womb-gathered pregnancy, partial conception, penetration, and detention for pregnancy verification form a concealed reproductive assembly. The body is both container and timed process.

#### Subchannel B. Readiness, mating, and uncertain fertility
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Female readiness and male performance are tested by whether conception actually follows.
- Active motifs: استئتاء الناقة (`quranic:root_000009:B012/m01`); قرء الناقة في الضبعة والوداق (`quranic:root_001210:B009/m01`); سرعة اللقاح (`quranic:root_001372:B003/m01`); فحل يكثر الضراب ولا يلقح (`quranic:root_001087:B003/m01`); لقاح جزئي أو حمل الناقة (`quranic:root_000819:B012/m01`); امرأة يرجى ولدها (`quranic:root_000969:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ل ق ي`: 69:20 (مُلَٰقٍ); `غ س ل`: 69:36 (غِسْلِينٍ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن)
- Synthesis: Mare in heat, camel mating-readiness, rapid conception, a frequently mating but infertile male, partial insemination, and a woman from whom children are hoped describe the uncertain transition from coupling to generation.

#### Subchannel C. Approaching birth, first growth, and ripened issue
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hidden process becomes externally legible as its time and product approach.
- Active motifs: قرب النتاج (`quranic:root_000493:B004/m01`); جن الشيء في بدايته (`quranic:root_000266:B014/m01`); دنو القِطاف وحلول وقته (`quranic:root_001241:B003/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); إطعام الغصن وقبول الوصل (`quranic:root_000934:B010/m01`); خروج النماء والنتاج (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); هناءة الماشية من النبت (`quranic:root_001604:B003/m01`); تتابع الخلق (`quranic:root_000934:B014/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `د ن و`: 69:23 (دَانِيَةٌ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا)
- Synthesis: Near delivery, the first stage of youth, harvest time, fruit taking flavor, successful graft, emerging produce, an animal's share of fresh growth, and successive formation all express maturation becoming available at the boundary.

#### Subchannel D. Offspring, age, and family naming
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: New life is classified by species, age, and social name.
- Active motifs: سلفان الحجل (`quranic:root_000733:B011/m01`); المرأة المسلف (`quranic:root_000733:B012/m01`); رضوى ورضيا أسماء (`quranic:root_000570:B003/m01`); رضوى ورضيا أعلام من المادة (`quranic:root_000569:B007/m01`); ولد الحجل والقطا (`quranic:root_000735:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ر ض ي`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ); `س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ)
- Synthesis: Partridge chicks, mature woman, personal and mountain names, and young sandgrouse classify generation after emergence by species, age, and social identity. The `ر ض ي` name motif lacks a surface-root anchor; its role here is lexical naming, not a claimed surface occurrence.

### 27. P2: Speech moves between utterance, belief, and verdict
- Semantic invariant: A statement can be voiced, circulated, negotiated, held inwardly, treated as a proposition, or imposed as judgment.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:19, 69:25, and 69:26 stage first-person utterance, public address, and retrospective knowledge.
- Surprising reach: Speech becomes tongue, reputation, bargaining, private thought, logical definition, song, excessive talk, and a sharp instrument of authority.

#### Subchannel A. Voiced statement and circulating talk
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A speaker externalizes words that pass among people as report or negotiation.
- Active motifs: إخراج القول بالنطق (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); اللسان آلة القول (`quranic:root_001272:B002/m01`); كثرة القول في صاحبه (`quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`); القول الفاشي بين الناس (`quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`); المقاولة في الأمر (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`); قراءة وتلاوة وإقراء (`quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`); الغِناء والصوت (`quranic:root_001110:B003/m01`); إفراط اللسان والكلام (`quranic:root_000512:B011/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا)
- Synthesis: Utterance, tongue, fluent speaker, circulating report, mutual discussion, recitation, song, and excessive speech form the audible social life of the statement. Reading the record is therefore one regulated form within a wider field of speech circulation.

#### Subchannel B. Fabricated attribution, inward statement, and proposition
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Speech may be falsely assigned, silently held, adopted as belief, or abstracted into a definition.
- Active motifs: قول ما لم يكن أو نسبته (`quranic:root_001272:B005/m01`); اجترار القول إلى النفس (`quranic:root_001272:B006/m01`); قول يجري مجرى الظن (`quranic:root_001272:B011/m01`); قول في النفس لم يظهر (`quranic:root_001272:B012/m01`); القول اعتقاد ومذهب (`quranic:root_001272:B013/m01`); قول الشيء دلالته (`quranic:root_001272:B014/m01`); قول الشيء حده (`quranic:root_001272:B016/m01`); شك وتوهم (`quranic:root_000969:B003/m01`); تهمة وظنة (`quranic:root_000969:B004/m01`); سوء الظن بالناس (`quranic:root_000969:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن)
- Synthesis: Putting words in another's mouth, appropriating a saying, using speech as conjecture, keeping it inward, holding a doctrine, letting a thing 'say' by sign, and defining its essence move speech away from simple sound. Doubt, accusation, and habitual suspicion expose the ethical risk in that expansion.

#### Subchannel C. Knowledge, search, and reception of instruction
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A knower seeks, discovers, and takes in information by inquiry or directed approach.
- Active motifs: الدراية والعلم (`quranic:root_000473:B001/m01`); قصد الشيء واعتماده (`quranic:root_000473:B002/m01`); الختل والاستتار للصيد (`quranic:root_000473:B003/m01`); المدرى والحد المحدد (`quranic:root_000473:B004/m01`); الحسبان والظن (`quranic:root_000318:B002/m01`); التحسب والاستخبار (`quranic:root_000318:B010/m01`); تلقي الكلام أخذا وتلقينا (`quranic:root_001372:B011/m01`); قارئ ناسك متفقه (`quranic:root_001210:B006/m01`); الشرب فهما (`quranic:root_000783:B011/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `د ر ي`: 69:26 (أَدْرِ); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `ل ق ي`: 69:20 (مُلَٰقٍ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ)
- Synthesis: Knowledge, targeted search, hidden approach, pointed tool, estimation, inquiry, verbal reception, learned devotional reading, and 'drinking' as understanding form an epistemic acquisition scene. Search can be intellectual or physically staged like a hunt, but both require directed attention and uptake.

#### Subchannel D. Ruling speech and the force of proof
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A claim becomes operative when backed by proof, office, and a conclusive decision.
- Active motifs: السلطان حجة قاهرة (`quranic:root_000732:B002/m01`); السلطان قدرة وولاية (`quranic:root_000732:B003/m01`); لسان سليط حاد صخاب (`quranic:root_000732:B004/m01`); الحكم والأمر القاطع (`quranic:root_001237:B001/m01`); الإبلاغ والوصية القاطعة (`quranic:root_001237:B002/m01`); القيل صاحب القول النافذ (`quranic:root_001272:B004/m01`); اقتالة الحكم على غيره (`quranic:root_001272:B010/m01`); إثبات يوجب حكما أو قدرا (`quranic:root_001283:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ل ط`: 69:29 (سُلْطَٰنِ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ)
- Synthesis: Prevailing proof, delegated authority, sharp tongue, adjudication, formal notice, authoritative speaker, imposed judgment, and written decree join discourse to enforcement. The verdict is speech whose social conditions make it binding.

#### Subchannel E. Particle, interrogative substitute, and letter-name
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Small grammatical forms orient clauses, questions, negations, and written sound.
- Active motifs: حرف عَلَى وما جرى مجراه (`quranic:root_001042:B012/m01`); ها بدل ألف الاستفهام (`quranic:root_001573:B004/m01`); الهاء حرف هجاء (`quranic:root_001573:B006/m01`); ليس جحود ينفي الحال كفعل جامد (`quranic:root_001390:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع ل و`: 69:22 (عَالِيَةٍ); `ه ا ء`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ل ي س`: 69:35 (لَيْسَ)
- Synthesis: The preposition `ala`, a deictic form replacing an interrogative alif, the name of the letter ha, and grammatical negation form a compact language-mechanics scene. The `ه ا ء` forms have no available surface-root anchor.

#### Subchannel F. Claim of unconsumed drink and contested conduct
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A speaker imputes an act to another, who must distinguish actual conduct from verbal accusation.
- Active motifs: دعوى ما لم يشرب (`quranic:root_000783:B010/m01`); قول ما لم يكن أو نسبته (`quranic:root_001272:B005/m01`); تهمة وظنة (`quranic:root_000969:B004/m01`); التحسب والاستخبار (`quranic:root_000318:B010/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ)
- Synthesis: The claim that another drank what they did not, fabricated attribution, accusation, and inquiry into the report create a compact dispute over conduct and evidence.

### 28. P2: Cloth is wrapped, lined, threaded, and worn
- Semantic invariant: Textile construction proceeds from joined material through inner lining, thread-path, cut edge, wrapping, and protective or deceptive surface.
- Surface relation: indirect; the book's joined leaves and the chain's serial construction activate craft operations adjacent to the surface judgment scene.
- Surprising reach: Garment making reaches waterskin stitching, shoe lining, plush bedding, hidden sword, under-armor layer, leather residue, and cloth whose colors misrepresent its state.

#### Subchannel A. Wrapping, underlayer, and hidden load
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Cloth encloses the body and creates an inner layer in which another object may be concealed.
- Active motifs: لف الثوب واشتماله (`quranic:root_000819:B003/m01`); حمل مستور في الاشتمال (`quranic:root_000819:B005/m01`); غلالة بين الثياب (`quranic:root_001102:B007/m01`); السلفة بطانة الخف (`quranic:root_000733:B013/m01`); القطيفة ذات الخمل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_001241:B007/m01`); الستر والاستتار (`quranic:root_000266:B001/m01`); الجُنّة الواقية (`quranic:root_000266:B008/m01`); مواراة الميت (`quranic:root_000266:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ)
- Synthesis: Cloak, object hidden under the cloak, undergarment, shoe lining, plush cover, generic concealment, shield, and burial covering organize textile by layers. Protection and concealment are neighboring but distinct uses of the same enclosing surface.

#### Subchannel B. Thread, joined leather, and extended edge
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Thread passes through joined material and continues as a stable seam or border.
- Active motifs: إدخال الشيء في الشيء (`quranic:root_000735:B002/m01`); الخيط الذي يخاط به (`quranic:root_000735:B004/m01`); طرة ممتدة في ناحية الثوب (`quranic:root_000735:B005/m01`); ضم شيء إلى شيء (`quranic:root_001283:B001/m01`); دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); بلى السقاء لطول الترك (`quranic:root_001237:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ); `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ)
- Synthesis: Insertion, sewing thread, long selvedge, joined leather, penetration between parts, and decayed waterskin form a complete seam-making scene. A worn container fails where the join no longer holds.

#### Subchannel C. Cut cloth, polished pattern, and misleading color
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A surface is cut and finished into a visible textile whose appearance may or may not disclose its substance.
- Active motifs: لف الثوب واشتماله (`quranic:root_000819:B003/m01`); القطيفة ذات الخمل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_001241:B007/m01`); بقية لحم في الإهاب (`quranic:root_001102:B012/m01`); لون الأحسب والأحسبية (`quranic:root_000318:B009/m01`); عود القال لضرب القلة (`quranic:root_001272:B008/m01`); طرة ممتدة في ناحية الثوب (`quranic:root_000735:B005/m01`); مخالطة اللون والقلب (`quranic:root_000783:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ)
- Synthesis: Wrapped cloth, pile fabric, flesh left on a hide, mottled coloring, color absorbed into material or heart, a wooden bat, and an extended textile edge place finish and appearance beside imperfect preparation. The scene pressures the book's legibility by asking whether a surface is well made or merely visually persuasive.

#### Subchannel D. Protective covering and bodily stilling
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A made covering shields a vulnerable body or arrests its movement.
- Active motifs: الجُنّة الواقية (`quranic:root_000266:B008/m01`); مواراة الميت (`quranic:root_000266:B009/m01`); إسكان الذبيحة بالسكين (`quranic:root_000726:B007/m01`); شد وعاء يشتمل على الحيوان أو النخلة (`quranic:root_000819:B004/m01`); طوق يغل العضو (`quranic:root_001102:B006/m01`); غلالة بين الثياب (`quranic:root_001102:B007/m01`); الأخذ بالذراع وتذريعه (`quranic:root_000512:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا)
- Synthesis: Shield, burial cover, knife, tied udder cover, iron collar, under-armor layer, and arm-restraint all use material at the body boundary. Protection and punishment diverge according to whether the covering intercepts harm or fixes the person inside it.

### 29. P2: Water is routed from channel to throat
- Semantic invariant: Water moves through runoff, channel, basin, vessel, throat, body, washing, and irrigated ground.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:24's command to drink and 69:35-36's punitive food create the intake contrast around which the hydrological imagery gathers.
- Surprising reach: The channel includes foreign floodwater, a ruddered vessel, honeycomb, smooth drink, saline water, seasoned skin, hidden valley stream, bath, and lush pasture.

#### Subchannel A. Runoff, channel, and incoming yield
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water arrives from elsewhere, is directed through a course, and produces growth at its destination.
- Active motifs: مجرى الماء وتسليك سبيله (`quranic:root_000009:B004/m01`); السيل الآتي من غير البلد (`quranic:root_000009:B005/m01`); خروج النماء والنتاج (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); ماء وموضع ونبت في الشجر (`quranic:root_001102:B003/m01`); أرض ونبت ريان (`quranic:root_000783:B014/m01`); البستان المستور بالشجر (`quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`); التفاف النبات واندفاعه (`quranic:root_000266:B011/m01`); السلوف في أوائل الإبل (`quranic:root_000733:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ)
- Synthesis: Channel, imported runoff, increased water or yield, hidden stream among trees, moist ground, garden, dense vegetation, and lead animal at the watering place form a routed irrigation scene. Arrival becomes productive because the flow is guided rather than merely abundant.

#### Subchannel B. Drink, share, vessel, and bodily passage
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Liquid is allotted, contained, taken through mouth and throat, and judged by its ease or bitterness.
- Active motifs: تناول المائع (`quranic:root_000783:B001/m01`); نصيب الماء (`quranic:root_000783:B002/m01`); جماعة الشرب والمشارب (`quranic:root_000783:B003/m01`); موضع الشرب وآلته (`quranic:root_000783:B004/m01`); ماء يشرب مع كراهة (`quranic:root_000783:B005/m01`); شارب الفم ومجاري الحلق (`quranic:root_000783:B006/m01`); مد العنق للنظر (`quranic:root_000783:B009/m01`); السلاسة في الجري والشرب (`quranic:root_000731:B001/m01`); وعاء الأكل وموضعه (`quranic:root_000043:B013/m01`); هناءة بلا مشقة ولا وخامة (`quranic:root_001604:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `س ل س ل`: 69:32 (سِلْسِلَةٍ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا)
- Synthesis: Drink, water-share, drinking cohort, room or cup, saline liquid, lip and throat channels, raised neck, smooth flow, bowl, and easy digestion trace liquid from social allocation into the body. Taste and posture reveal whether the passage is welcome or forced.

#### Subchannel C. Seasoned skin, filter, and washing place
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A vessel is sealed and filtered, then used to pour or wash.
- Active motifs: تطييب القربة وتشريبها (`quranic:root_000783:B013/m01`); خرقة تحبس صب الماء (`quranic:root_001102:B008/m01`); تطهير الشيء بإسالة الماء وإزالة الدرن (`quranic:root_001087:B001/m01`); ماء الغسل وما يغسل به أو فيه (`quranic:root_001087:B002/m01`); الماء الحار والاغتسال به (`quranic:root_000001:B002/m01`); موضع الاغتسال وآلة التسخين (`quranic:root_000001:B013/m01`); بلى السقاء لطول الترك (`quranic:root_001237:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `غ س ل`: 69:36 (غِسْلِينٍ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ)
