- Synthesis: Seasoning closes a waterskin's stitches; a cloth filter controls its spout; flowing cleanser, washing medium, hot water, bath vessel, and an old skin that has decayed complete the container's life cycle from preparation to failure.

#### Subchannel D. Vessel, hive, and stabilizing rudder
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hollow structure carries or stores fluid products and is kept from uncontrolled motion.
- Active motifs: الخلية السفينة (`quranic:root_000436:B011/m01`); خلية النحل (`quranic:root_000436:B012/m01`); تسكين السفينة بالسكان (`quranic:root_000726:B008/m01`); مجرى الماء وتسليك سبيله (`quranic:root_000009:B004/m01`); امتداد ملتف متعاقب (`quranic:root_000731:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `س ل س ل`: 69:32 (سِلْسِلَةٍ)
- Synthesis: Large vessel, bee-hive, ship's steering or balancing member, water channel, and chain-like lightning or sand all use guided extension around a hollow or moving body. The rudder supplies the key operation: stability within a fluid environment.

### 30. P2: Punishment is mapped onto limbs and inner organs
- Semantic invariant: Control and suffering are localized at hand, arm, neck, throat, back, eye, belly, bone, and breath.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:30-32 physically takes, fetters, burns, measures, and inserts the condemned body.
- Surprising reach: The anatomy reaches speech organs, a padded seat, hip enlargement, eye inflammation, vomiting, colic, and the amount a forearm can physically span.

#### Subchannel A. Hand, forearm, neck, and choke
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: External force controls the body through its grasping and load-bearing joints.
- Active motifs: الذراع جارحة ممدودة (`quranic:root_000512:B001/m01`); القياس بالذراع (`quranic:root_000512:B002/m01`); الذرع طاقة ومدى (`quranic:root_000512:B004/m01`); الأخذ بالذراع وتذريعه (`quranic:root_000512:B007/m01`); على حبل الذراع حاضر (`quranic:root_000512:B009/m01`); خفيف اليد في العمل (`quranic:root_000512:B010/m01`); طوق يغل العضو (`quranic:root_001102:B006/m01`); الأخذ بالمطعمة عند الخنق (`quranic:root_000934:B012/m01`); إدخال الحبل في العنق (`quranic:root_000783:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ)
- Synthesis: Arm, cubit, capacity, arm-grip, immediate arm's reach, skilled hand, iron collar, throat-seizure, and neck-rope map restraint through connected limbs. Measurement and control meet at the forearm, which is both bodily lever and standard of length.

#### Subchannel B. Mouth, throat, speech, and forced passage
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The same passage carries drink, food, voice, and constricting pressure.
- Active motifs: شارب الفم ومجاري الحلق (`quranic:root_000783:B006/m01`); مد العنق للنظر (`quranic:root_000783:B009/m01`); الشرب فهما (`quranic:root_000783:B011/m01`); ذوق الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000934:B001/m01`); طعم العقل والقيمة (`quranic:root_000934:B008/m01`); الأخذ بالمطعمة عند الخنق (`quranic:root_000934:B012/m01`); التطاعم بالفم (`quranic:root_000934:B013/m01`); إفراط اللسان والكلام (`quranic:root_000512:B011/m01`); لسان سليط حاد صخاب (`quranic:root_000732:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `س ل ط`: 69:29 (سُلْطَٰنِ)
- Synthesis: Lip, throat channels, raised neck, understanding, taste, reason, throat-grip, mouth-to-mouth contact, excessive talk, and sharp tongue turn the oral passage into a contested interface. What normally communicates and nourishes can be constricted or compelled.

#### Subchannel C. Back, bone, eye, and altered bodily profile
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Hard support and visible contour register strain, disease, or artificial modification.
- Active motifs: الصَّلا موضع الظهر والذنب (`quranic:root_000880:B006/m01`); المصلي يتلو السابق (`quranic:root_000880:B007/m01`); مستغلظ العضو (`quranic:root_001029:B003/m01`); العَظْم الصلب (`quranic:root_001029:B005/m01`); عظامة الردف (`quranic:root_001029:B008/m01`); خشبة الرحل (`quranic:root_001029:B009/m01`); العين المتوقدة أو الجاحظة (`quranic:root_000225:B003/m01`); المحسبة والوسادة (`quranic:root_000318:B008/m01`); لون الأحسب والأحسبية (`quranic:root_000318:B009/m01`); دنو الأعلى من الوسط (`quranic:root_000493:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ص ل ي`: 69:31 (صَلُّ); `ع ظ م`: 69:33 (عَظِيمِ); `ج ح م`: 69:31 (جَحِيمَ); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `د ن و`: 69:23 (دَانِيَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Lower back, runner behind the leader, thick limb, bone, padded rump, saddle wood, inflamed or staring eye, cushion, mottled skin, and stooped upper body produce a body read through support and profile. Punishment deforms the same structural system that normally carries and seats.

#### Subchannel D. Heat, vomiting, colic, and failed interior control
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Internal pressure overcomes the body's ability to contain fluid, food, or breath.
- Active motifs: العرق وأثر الحر (`quranic:root_000001:B004/m01`); الحمى والحمام في الأبدان والأرض (`quranic:root_000001:B005/m01`); سبق الشيء وغلبته (`quranic:root_000512:B006/m01`); حرارة تنغل في الجوف (`quranic:root_001102:B002/m01`); تلهب الوجه بالغضب (`quranic:root_000225:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ج ح م`: 69:31 (جَحِيمَ)
- Synthesis: Sweat, fever, involuntary vomiting, inward burning thirst, and a face flaring with anger describe heat and pressure breaching inner regulation.

#### Subchannel E. Face, neck, foreskin, and surface wound
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Named bodily surfaces are exposed to distortion, cutting, or abrasion.
- Active motifs: عوج يأخذ الوجه (`quranic:root_001372:B001/m01`); السالفة من العنق (`quranic:root_000733:B007/m01`); السلف القلفة (`quranic:root_000733:B009/m01`); خدش الوجه ونحوه (`quranic:root_001241:B006/m01`); أسماء بعلامة الذراع (`quranic:root_000512:B012/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ل ق ي`: 69:20 (مُلَٰقٍ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا)
- Synthesis: Facial palsy, front or side of the neck, foreskin, facial scratch, and names derived from an arm-mark map bodily identity through exposed surfaces and their alteration.

#### Subchannel F. Epidemic, madness, and bitter medicine
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Disease can spread through a place, cover the mind, and call for an aversive treatment.
- Active motifs: قرأة البلاد ووبؤها (`quranic:root_001210:B008/m01`); ستر العقل بالجنون (`quranic:root_000266:B006/m01`); الحُضُض دواء مر (`quranic:root_000334:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ح ض ض`: 69:34 (يَحُضُّ)
- Synthesis: Regional epidemic, mind covered by madness, and bitter medicinal resin form a compact diagnostic-treatment scene spanning place, cognition, and remedy.

### 31. P2: Arrival resolves into encounter, route, or stoppage
- Semantic invariant: Movement has three decisive moments: the way by which something comes, the meeting at its end, and the condition that permits or prevents further passage.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:19-32 moves records and persons through giving, meeting, seizure, fire, and insertion.
- Surprising reach: The route expands into a public road, camel foreleg motion, straight thrust, cast-off object, slow gait, illness that breaks travel, and residence that outlasts movement.

#### Subchannel A. Coming, road, and foreign entrant
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A mover enters by a known way, sometimes as outsider, payment, produce, or calamity.
- Active motifs: الإتيان والمجيء (`quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`); الغريب الداخل في غير قومه (`quranic:root_000009:B006/m01`); الإتاوة المؤداة (`quranic:root_000009:B008/m01`); رجع يدي الناقة في السير (`quranic:root_000009:B009/m01`); الميتاء طريق ومحاذاة (`quranic:root_000009:B010/m01`); إتيان البلاء والهلاك (`quranic:root_000009:B011/m01`); نَفاذ الرجل (`quranic:root_000009:B013/m01`); النفاذ في الطريق (`quranic:root_000735:B001/m01`); الطعنة المستقيمة تلقاء الوجه (`quranic:root_000735:B003/m01`); مشي الدابة المتقارب البطيء (`quranic:root_001241:B005/m01`); القصد والطلب نحو الشيء (`quranic:root_000001:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ)
- Synthesis: Arrival, outsider, tribute, camel foreleg return, public road, disaster that overtakes, penetrating man, road passage, straight thrust, close slow gait, and directed intent distinguish kinds of coming by agent, path, and force.

#### Subchannel B. Meeting, throwing, and joining endpoints
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Two trajectories meet, or an object is cast so that it comes to lie before another.
- Active motifs: توافي شيئين متقابلين (`quranic:root_001372:B004/m01`); طرح الشيء حتى يلقى (`quranic:root_001372:B005/m01`); شيء مطروح متروك (`quranic:root_001372:B006/m01`); ما يلقاه المرء من خير أو شر (`quranic:root_001372:B007/m01`); انبطاح على القفا (`quranic:root_001372:B009/m01`); جعل طرفين يلتقيان (`quranic:root_001372:B010/m01`); الإيتاء والإعطاء (`quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`); حوز الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ل ق ي`: 69:20 (مُلَٰقٍ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:30 (خُذُ)
- Synthesis: Encounter, casting, discarded object, received good or harm, lying on the back, and bringing two ends together form the terminal geometry of motion. Giving and taking specify the social relation at that meeting point.

#### Subchannel C. Capacity, break in travel, and permanent station
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Movement stops because a body reaches its limit, a route fails, or residence becomes fixed.
- Active motifs: امتداد الخطو والسعي (`quranic:root_000512:B003/m01`); الذرع طاقة ومدى (`quranic:root_000512:B004/m01`); أمر لا يوثق بعاقبته (`quranic:root_000969:B006/m01`); ضعف وقلة حيلة (`quranic:root_000969:B008/m01`); الغنى بالمكان (`quranic:root_001110:B004/m01`); ذهاب الحركة (`quranic:root_000726:B001/m01`); استيطان المنزل (`quranic:root_000726:B002/m01`); موضع الاستقرار (`quranic:root_000726:B009/m01`); استهلاك الجهد واستفراغه (`quranic:root_001596:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ه ل ك`: 69:29 (هَلَكَ)
- Synthesis: Long stride and bodily capacity confront uncertain outcome, weakness, settled dwelling, stillness, house, stable station, and self-consuming effort. Stoppage can be chosen habitation or the forced end of an exhausted journey.

#### Subchannel D. Borderland, byway, and directional side
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Travel is located by the edge between settled and open land and by the side from which movement proceeds.
- Active motifs: النواحي ومشارف العمران (`quranic:root_000512:B008/m01`); أعلام وأجزاء تسمى بالذراع (`quranic:root_000512:B013/m01`); جهة الشمال خلاف اليمين (`quranic:root_000819:B006/m01`); ريح الشمال وما تبرده (`quranic:root_000819:B007/m01`); الميتاء طريق ومحاذاة (`quranic:root_000009:B010/m01`); النفاذ في الطريق (`quranic:root_000735:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `س ل ك`: 69:32 (ٱسْلُكُ)
- Synthesis: Outskirts near houses and palms, named arm-like landmarks, leftward side, north wind, public route, and traversable path create an oriented border landscape. The body supplies measures and directional terms for geography.

### 32. P2: Time advances from prior act to final due point
- Semantic invariant: Earlier deed, recurring interval, ripening, due time, and termination form a causal chronology.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:24 explicitly connects present ease to what was sent ahead in past days, while 69:27 longs for a final ending.
- Surprising reach: Temporal order is materialized as menstruation, animal heat, harvest, advance meal, debt maturity, fever's due moment, and waterskin decay.

#### Subchannel A. Precedence, elapsed days, and stored consequence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: What occurs earlier remains causally available when the later outcome arrives.
- Active motifs: التقدم والسبق (`quranic:root_000733:B001/m01`); السلفة قبل الطعام (`quranic:root_000733:B003/m01`); المال المقدم (`quranic:root_000733:B005/m01`); مضي الزمن وذهاب القرن (`quranic:root_000436:B003/m01`); وقت النهار المحدود (`quranic:root_001700:B001/m01`); مدة من الزمان (`quranic:root_001700:B002/m01`); كائنة اليوم وشدته (`quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`); الإتيان والمجيء (`quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`); إتيان البلاء والهلاك (`quranic:root_000009:B011/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ); `ي و م`: 69:24 (أَيَّامِ), 69:35 (يَوْمَ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ)
- Synthesis: Prior action, advance meal, prepaid capital, elapsed generations, ordinary day, extended duration, severe day, arrival, and overtaking calamity organize before and after. The later scene is the arrival of what the earlier act placed in motion.

#### Subchannel B. Bodily and agricultural intervals
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Recurring biological and seasonal times regulate readiness, maturation, and harvest.
- Active motifs: قرء الحيض والطهر (`quranic:root_001210:B003/m01`); وقت يحين أو يتأخر (`quranic:root_001210:B005/m01`); قرء الناقة في الضبعة والوداق (`quranic:root_001210:B009/m01`); وقت الشيء ودورانه (`quranic:root_001211:B002/m01`); دنو القِطاف وحلول وقته (`quranic:root_001241:B003/m01`); قرب النتاج (`quranic:root_000493:B004/m01`); استئتاء الناقة (`quranic:root_000009:B012/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `د ن و`: 69:23 (دَانِيَةٌ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ)
- Synthesis: Menstrual or pure interval, wind-time, mating readiness, harvest time, near birth, and animal heat make time legible through changing bodies and crops. These are clocks whose units are states rather than numerals.

#### Subchannel C. Due moment, completion, and death
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A process reaches the point at which its result is fixed and no further extension remains.
- Active motifs: دنو الأمر وحضوره (`quranic:root_000001:B006/m01`); القضاء والقدر والموت (`quranic:root_000001:B010/m01`); المنية والقضاء النافذ بالموت (`quranic:root_001237:B003/m01`); الفراغ والتمام والانقضاء (`quranic:root_001237:B004/m01`); إحكام الصنع وإنفاذ الفعل (`quranic:root_001237:B005/m01`); أمر لا يوثق بعاقبته (`quranic:root_000969:B006/m01`); الشمال موضع شؤم أو خساسة (`quranic:root_000819:B013/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ)
- Synthesis: Due arrival, decreed death, mortality, completion, executed making, uncertain outcome, and failed lot converge on finalization. The wish for the decisive ending is a wish that the temporal process had terminated before its consequences unfolded.

#### Subchannel D. Aging, first stage, and deteriorated material
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Time appears in bodies and artifacts as youth, maturity, age, and decay.
- Active motifs: جن الشيء في بدايته (`quranic:root_000266:B014/m01`); المرأة المسلف (`quranic:root_000733:B012/m01`); الشيخ المنسوب إلى كُنْتُ (`quranic:root_001332:B005/m01`); بلى السقاء لطول الترك (`quranic:root_001237:B007/m01`); الحمى والحمام في الأبدان والأرض (`quranic:root_000001:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ك و ن`: 69:27 (كَانَتِ), 69:33 (كَانَ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ)
- Synthesis: Beginning of youth, mature woman, elder who speaks in the past tense, decayed waterskin, and fevered body show time as alteration of capacity and material integrity.

### 33. P2: Social rank is built from household, approval, and reputation
- Semantic invariant: Standing in a community depends on household attachment, recognized character, mutual approval, public reputation, and the ability to provide.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:21-24 portrays accepted well-being, while 69:28-35 removes wealth, authority, companion, and provision.
- Surprising reach: The field includes householder, poor resident, divorced woman, in-law, guarantor, singer, sharp speaker, honor inherited from ancestors, and a mountain used as a personal name.

#### Subchannel A. Household, spouse, and familiar rest
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A stable domestic unit joins residence, spouse, dependents, and emotional repose.
- Active motifs: استيطان المنزل (`quranic:root_000726:B002/m01`); أهل الدار (`quranic:root_000726:B003/m01`); مأنس السكون (`quranic:root_000726:B004/m01`); الانفراد والخلوة (`quranic:root_000436:B002/m01`); الخلو من الزوج أو الولد (`quranic:root_000436:B009/m01`); الغانية المستغنية (`quranic:root_001110:B005/m01`); الغنى والتزويج (`quranic:root_001110:B006/m01`); سلف المصاهرة (`quranic:root_000733:B006/m01`); ائتلاف الشمل بعد تفرق (`quranic:root_000819:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ)
- Synthesis: House, household, familiar comfort, privacy, divorced status, self-sufficient wife, marriage as protection, intermarried relatives, and reunited family define domestic stability by the relations that can also be lost.

#### Subchannel B. Approval, obedience, guarantee, and named identity
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person is socially received through consent, trusted service, guarantee, and public naming.
- Active motifs: الرضا خلاف السخط (`quranic:root_000569:B001/m01`); الرضوان والمرضاة اسم للرضا الكثير أو المطلوب (`quranic:root_000569:B002/m01`); المراضاة والتراضي رضا متبادل (`quranic:root_000569:B003/m01`); الإرضاء طلب رضا الغير وإزالة سخطه (`quranic:root_000569:B004/m01`); راضاني فرضوته غلبة في ذلك (`quranic:root_000569:B005/m01`); الرضي صفة للمطيع أو المحب أو الضامن (`quranic:root_000569:B006/m01`); رضوى ورضيا أعلام من المادة (`quranic:root_000569:B007/m01`); الرِّضا خلاف السخط والقبول (`quranic:root_000570:B001/m01`); غلبة راضاني فرضوته (`quranic:root_000570:B002/m01`); رضوى ورضيا أسماء (`quranic:root_000570:B003/m01`); الرَّضِيّ طاعة ومحبة وضمان (`quranic:root_000570:B004/m01`); تهنئة بخير وولاية (`quranic:root_001604:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ); `ر ض ي`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا)
- Synthesis: Acceptance, abundant favor, mutual consent, active pleasing, competitive approval, obedient or guaranteeing associate, and personal names organize social reception. Congratulation publicly ratifies the good state. All `ر ض ي` motifs are lexically attested but surface-root anchors are unavailable.

#### Subchannel C. Reputation, song, fluent speech, and inherited honor
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Public standing is produced and circulated through voice, ancestry, and visible accomplishment.
- Active motifs: الحسب والمآثر (`quranic:root_000318:B004/m01`); الغِناء والصوت (`quranic:root_001110:B003/m01`); لسان سليط حاد صخاب (`quranic:root_000732:B004/m01`); كثرة القول في صاحبه (`quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`); القول الفاشي بين الناس (`quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`); الحرمة والشرف (`quranic:root_001029:B010/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `س ل ط`: 69:29 (سُلْطَٰنِ); `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ); `ع ظ م`: 69:33 (عَظِيمِ)
- Synthesis: Ancestral honor, song, forceful tongue, verbal fluency, circulating report, and social dignity make reputation an acoustic and genealogical asset. Its loss parallels the disappearance of wealth and authority.

#### Subchannel D. Crowd, dependent property, and provision-bearing group
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: People, dependents, and livestock gather as a resource-bearing social body.
- Active motifs: سواد الناس وجماعتهم (`quranic:root_000266:B013/m01`); القِرَة مال وعيال (`quranic:root_001210:B013/m01`); الحميم والحامة في القربى (`quranic:root_000001:B011/m01`); كرائم المال والإبل (`quranic:root_000001:B014/m01`); أهل الدار (`quranic:root_000726:B003/m01`); قوت يثبت المقام (`quranic:root_000726:B010/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ)
- Synthesis: Mass of people, property and dependents, close kin, choice stock, household, sustaining pasture, and livelihood describe a group whose bonds are simultaneously emotional and economic.

### 34. P2: Life is experienced as condition and station
- Semantic invariant: Living is not bare existence but a condition sustained by food, drink, place, household, and the capacity to stand for another.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:21 names a satisfying life, while 69:27 and 69:33 use being to recall former condition and conduct.
- Surprising reach: Life becomes livelihood, dwelling, social rank, bad condition, guarantorship, submission, and the elder whose identity is narrated through what he once was.

#### Subchannel A. Life, livelihood, and satisfying condition
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A living state is maintained by the means and quality of daily existence.
- Active motifs: الحياة والعيشة (`quranic:root_001067:B001/m01`); المعيشة والمعاش (`quranic:root_001067:B002/m01`); الرضا خلاف السخط (`quranic:root_000569:B001/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`); مال يؤكل ويشرب (`quranic:root_000783:B012/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع ي ش`: 69:21 (عِيشَةٍ); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ)
- Synthesis: Life, livelihood, accepted condition, provision, and wealth expended on food and drink define flourishing through sustained means. Satisfaction describes the quality of a materially supported life.

#### Subchannel B. Being, place, rank, and bad state
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Existence appears as occurring in time, occupying a place, holding a station, or enduring a degraded condition.
- Active motifs: وقوع الشيء وحضوره في زمان (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`); المكان والمكانة من الكون (`quranic:root_001332:B002/m01`); حالة السوء بكينة (`quranic:root_001332:B006/m01`); الغنى بالمكان (`quranic:root_001110:B004/m01`); مظنة الشيء وموضعه (`quranic:root_000969:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك و ن`: 69:27 (كَانَتِ), 69:33 (كَانَ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن)
- Synthesis: Occurrence, place and rank, bad condition, long residence, and the expected location of a thing turn being into situated presence. Condition can therefore be read from where and how one remains.

#### Subchannel C. Standing for another and submission
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: One person becomes answerable for another, or lowers themselves beneath an authority.
- Active motifs: الكفالة والقيام على فلان (`quranic:root_001332:B003/m01`); الخضوع بالاستكانة (`quranic:root_001332:B004/m01`); الرضي صفة للمطيع أو المحب أو الضامن (`quranic:root_000569:B006/m01`); ذل المسكنة (`quranic:root_000726:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك و ن`: 69:27 (كَانَتِ), 69:33 (كَانَ); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ)
- Synthesis: Guarantee, submission, obedient or guaranteeing associate, and humbled poverty describe dependence through altered standing. The same social posture may be responsible care or coerced abasement.

### 35. P2: Vertical rank moves between dominance and recovery
- Semantic invariant: Height can signify honor, coercive superiority, an upper tool-position, a summons upward, or recovery from a lowered bodily state.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:22 locates the garden high, while 69:33 names divine greatness and 69:34 the socially lowly poor person.
- Surprising reach: The vertical field reaches tyrannical ascent, mastery, a call to approach, anvil and spear-tip, prepositional 'upon,' low ground at a mountain foot, baseness, and rising after illness.

#### Subchannel A. Arrogant ascent and coercive superiority
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An agent turns elevation into mastery over others.
- Active motifs: العظمة والتجبر (`quranic:root_001042:B003/m01`); الغلبة والاستيلاء (`quranic:root_001042:B004/m01`); السلاطة قوة قاهرة مسلطة (`quranic:root_000732:B001/m01`); السلطان قدرة وولاية (`quranic:root_000732:B003/m01`); الدناءة والضعة (`quranic:root_000493:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع ل و`: 69:22 (عَالِيَةٍ); `س ل ط`: 69:29 (سُلْطَٰنِ); `د ن و`: 69:23 (دَانِيَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Arrogant elevation, dominance, coercive capacity, delegated rule, and social baseness establish a hierarchy whose upper and lower positions are ethically charged.

#### Subchannel B. Upward summons and elevated implement
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An upper point or tool-position organizes approach and work.
- Active motifs: نداء التعالي (`quranic:root_001042:B006/m01`); أسماء الأدوات والأجزاء المرتفعة (`quranic:root_001042:B009/m01`); حرف عَلَى وما جرى مجراه (`quranic:root_001042:B012/m01`); الحَضيض قرار الأرض (`quranic:root_000334:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع ل و`: 69:22 (عَالِيَةٍ); `ح ض ض`: 69:34 (يَحُضُّ)
- Synthesis: The call 'come up,' anvil or raised spear-part, the preposition 'upon,' and low ground at a mountain foot form a vertical coordinate system. Command, tool, grammar, and terrain all locate action relative to upper and lower.

#### Subchannel C. Recovery as rising from illness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A body leaves a lowered state and regains health and standing.
- Active motifs: السلامة من النفاس أو العلة (`quranic:root_001042:B011/m01`); الإرضاء طلب رضا الغير وإزالة سخطه (`quranic:root_000569:B004/m01`); الفراغ والتمام والانقضاء (`quranic:root_001237:B004/m01`); هناءة بلا مشقة ولا وخامة (`quranic:root_001604:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع ل و`: 69:22 (عَالِيَةٍ); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا)
- Synthesis: Recovery after childbirth or illness, active pleasing, completion of a condition, and untroubled well-being describe healing as ascent out of impairment.

### 36. P2: Hunting turns landscape into trap and prey
- Semantic invariant: A hunter uses concealment, lure, implement, and terrain to convert an animal into available food.
- Surface relation: indirect; the punitive food scene activates the lexical distinction between nourishment, prey, and the mechanisms that procure it.
- Surprising reach: The scene reaches camel-screen, prey-producing bow, snare, prepared victim, snake, pigeon, cut grass, pasture plant, and edible leafy growth.

#### Subchannel A. Hunter's screen, feeding bow, snare, and prey
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hunter conceals approach, sets or uses an implement, and captures an animal for food.
- Active motifs: الذريعة سبب وسترة (`quranic:root_000512:B005/m01`); آلة الصيد التي تطعم صاحبها (`quranic:root_000934:B006/m01`); المَصالي أشراك وفخوخ (`quranic:root_000880:B005/m01`); الفريسة والمعدة للأكل (`quranic:root_000043:B007/m01`); السبع الحيوان المفترس (`quranic:root_000669:B002/m01`); السلوف من النصال (`quranic:root_000733:B014/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ص ل ي`: 69:31 (صَلُّ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ); `س ب ع`: 69:32 (سَبْعُونَ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ)
- Synthesis: Animal used as a shooting screen, bow or raptor that feeds its owner, trap, prepared prey, predator, and long arrowhead form a complete hunting mechanism from approach to captured food.

#### Subchannel B. Snake, pigeon, grass, and pasture edge
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Small animals move through the vegetation that feeds or conceals them.
- Active motifs: الجان حية (`quranic:root_000266:B012/m01`); الحمام الطائر (`quranic:root_000001:B012/m01`); الخلى والحشيش المقطوع (`quranic:root_000436:B013/m01`); الصِّليان نبت ترعاه الإبل (`quranic:root_000880:B010/m01`); نبات رخص عريض الورق (`quranic:root_001241:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ); `ص ل ي`: 69:31 (صَلُّ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ)
- Synthesis: Snake, pigeon, cut fresh grass, pasture plant, and edible broad-leaf growth form a field-edge ecology. The same vegetation may be forage, cover, or gathered food depending on the participant.

#### Subchannel C. Animal and implement named by bodily shape
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A visible bodily proportion supplies names for animal, vessel, or tool.
- Active motifs: أسماء بعلامة الذراع (`quranic:root_000512:B012/m01`); اسم على شكل أو هيئة مخصوصة (`quranic:root_001372:B002/m01`); السالفة من العنق (`quranic:root_000733:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا); `ل ق ي`: 69:20 (مُلَٰقٍ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ)
- Synthesis: Names derived from an arm-mark, the hooked eagle or bucket form, and the front or side of the neck classify objects by bodily silhouette. Shape becomes a practical naming instrument within the hunting landscape.

### 37. P3: The oath spans sight, insight, and certain truth
- Semantic invariant: The assertion is secured across sensory visibility, inward recognition, sworn testimony, and knowledge from which doubt has been removed.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:38-39 swears by the visible and invisible, 69:49 declares divine knowledge of deniers, and 69:51 names the truth of certainty.
- Surprising reach: Evidence is materialized as blood-trace, shield, stitched hide, road-mark, and bright stone, so seeing becomes a practice of reading surfaces and protections.

#### Subchannel A. Eye, inward sight, and hidden report
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Perception moves from opened eye to penetrating understanding and then confronts claims about what cannot be seen.
- Active motifs: إبصار العين (`quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`); بصيرة القلب (`quranic:root_000121:B002/m01`); علم دقيق وفطنة (`quranic:root_000798:B005/m01`); انكشاف الشيء للعارف (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); الكاهن المتكهن بالأخبار الخفية (`quranic:root_001327:B001/m01`); تصديق يطمئن إليه القلب (`quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ب ص ر`: 69:38 (تُبْصِرُ), 69:39 (تُبْصِرُ); `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `ك ه ن`: 69:42 (كَاهِنٍ); `ء م ن`: 69:41 (تُؤْمِنُ)
- Synthesis: Ocular sight, inward insight, fine awareness, disclosed knowledge, divinatory report, and believing assent distinguish modes of access to reality. The hidden is not collapsed into the visible; rather, the source and reliability of each report become the issue.

#### Subchannel B. Blood trace, shield, seam, and readable surface
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A surface bears marks or joins that disclose injury, protection, or construction to an examiner.
- Active motifs: بصيرة الدم (`quranic:root_000121:B004/m01`); بصيرة السلاح (`quranic:root_000121:B005/m01`); غلظ الحافة ووصل الشقتين (`quranic:root_000121:B006/m01`); حجارة بصرة رخوة (`quranic:root_000121:B007/m01`); أثر يميز الشيء ويهدي إليه (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`); شق ظاهر في الشفة العليا (`quranic:root_001040:B004/m01`); علامة مشعرة وشعيرة نسك (`quranic:root_000798:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ب ص ر`: 69:38 (تُبْصِرُ), 69:39 (تُبْصِرُ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ)
- Synthesis: Blood trail, shield, thick edge, stitched skins, bright soft stone, guiding mark, split upper lip, and ritual or battle sign create a forensic field. Surfaces do not merely appear; they carry evidence of what happened and how the body or object was made.

#### Subchannel C. Sworn division and distributed testimony
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An oath allocates responsibility and binds speakers to a claim before an audience.
- Active motifs: إفراز النصيب وتقسيم الشيء (`quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`); يمين مقسومة على أهلها (`quranic:root_001226:B004/m01`); بال مقسم بين وجوه الأمر (`quranic:root_001226:B006/m01`); هدنة قسامة (`quranic:root_001226:B008/m01`); يمين الحلف (`quranic:root_001698:B003/m01`); إخراج القول بالنطق (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); استغراق النفي (`quranic:root_000017:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ); `ي م ن`: 69:45 (يَمِينِ); `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ)
- Synthesis: Division into shares, oath, deliberative apportionment, truce, sworn pledge, voiced statement, and all-inclusive 'anyone' place testimony in a social procedure. Responsibility is distributed, but the uttered claim binds every participating voice.

#### Subchannel D. Fixed reality and certainty beyond doubt
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A proposition is verified, made binding, and known with a stability that displaces doubt.
- Active motifs: ثبات مطابق للواقع ضد الباطل (`quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`); لزوم واجب واستحقاق ثابت (`quranic:root_000347:B002/m01`); إثبات الحق وإظهاره (`quranic:root_000347:B005/m01`); ثبات العلم وزوال الشك (`quranic:root_001696:B001/m01`); تصديق يطمئن إليه القلب (`quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`); انكشاف الشيء للعارف (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); حجب الحق (`quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ق ق`: 69:51 (حَقُّ); `ي ق ن`: 69:51 (يَقِينِ); `ء م ن`: 69:41 (تُؤْمِنُ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ)
- Synthesis: Reality, obligation, demonstration, settled certainty, assent, knowledge, and the act of covering truth form the final epistemic contrast. Certainty is not intensity alone: it is the convergence of true object, valid proof, receptive knower, and failed concealment.

### 38. P3: Authorized speech descends through a trusted carrier
- Semantic invariant: A valid message has an originating authority, an ordered descent, an honored bearer, and a speech act that preserves rather than invents its charge.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:40 identifies the speech of an honored messenger, and 69:43 identifies its descent from the Lord of the worlds.
- Surprising reach: Transmission is rendered as release, carried saying, measured gait, repeated descent, assigned rank, guest-provision, and a shipmaster who keeps the course.

#### Subchannel A. Messenger, carried saying, and measured delivery
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A bearer receives an utterance and conveys it with deliberate pace and fidelity.
- Active motifs: الإرسال والانبعاث (`quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`); الرسول والرسالة (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); السير السهل واللين (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); الرفق والتؤدة (`quranic:root_000563:B004/m01`); المراسلة والمسايرة (`quranic:root_000563:B008/m01`); إخراج القول بالنطق (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); الشرف والجود المحمود (`quranic:root_001294:B001/m01`); العزيز الذي يكرم عليك (`quranic:root_001294:B010/m01`); رباني الملاحين (`quranic:root_000532:B017/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر س ل`: 69:40 (رَسُولٍ); `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ); `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ)
- Synthesis: Dispatch, messenger, carried statement, easy gait, deliberation, paired performance, voiced saying, honor, dear entrusted object, and captaincy create a reliable delivery scene. The bearer is valued because movement, speech, and custodial role remain aligned.

#### Subchannel B. Descent, placement, and repeated revelation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Content moves from higher source to lower reception and is placed in its proper rank.
- Active motifs: هبوط الشيء وحلوله (`quranic:root_001492:B001/m01`); إنزال الشيء وإيصاله (`quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`); وضع الشيء في منزلته (`quranic:root_001492:B004/m01`); النزلة الواحدة (`quranic:root_001492:B010/m01`); ربوبية وملك وسيادة (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); إصلاح وتربية وإتمام (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); انكشاف الشيء للعارف (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); العلو والارتفاع (`quranic:root_000745:B001/m01`); السماء وما علا فأظل (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ن ز ل`: 69:43 (تَنزِيلٌ); `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ)
- Synthesis: Descent, caused descent, ordered placement, single descent-event, lordship, gradual cultivation, knowledge, height, and sky establish a vertical transmission process. Repetition does not weaken the source; it distributes the content in an ordered way appropriate to the receiver.

#### Subchannel C. Prepared reception and honored provision
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A recipient is treated like a guest for whom a suitable place and provision have been prepared.
- Active motifs: المنزل والمنزلة (`quranic:root_001492:B003/m01`); النزل المعد للنازل (`quranic:root_001492:B005/m01`); الشرف والجود المحمود (`quranic:root_001294:B001/m01`); هدية تطلب المكافأة (`quranic:root_001294:B008/m01`); جواب الرضا والكرامة (`quranic:root_001294:B009/m01`); الرخاء وطيب الإعطاء (`quranic:root_000563:B010/m01`); إصلاح وتربية وإتمام (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ن ز ل`: 69:43 (تَنزِيلٌ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ); `ر س ل`: 69:40 (رَسُولٍ); `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ)
- Synthesis: Dwelling and rank, guest-provision, generosity, gift seeking return, gracious response, ease of giving, and careful cultivation frame revelation as prepared reception. The image pressures the audience: refusing the message resembles refusing provision fitted to the guest.

#### Subchannel D. Bearer's honor versus competitive self-display
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: True honor rests in the entrusted role, while rivalry seeks to manufacture rank through comparison.
- Active motifs: الشرف والجود المحمود (`quranic:root_001294:B001/m01`); مفاخرة الكرم والغلبة فيه (`quranic:root_001294:B006/m01`); العزيز الذي يكرم عليك (`quranic:root_001294:B010/m01`); المساماة والمباراة (`quranic:root_000745:B007/m01`); الصيت الحسن المنتشر (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`); التعاظم والزهو (`quranic:root_001029:B006/m01`); الحرمة والشرف (`quranic:root_001029:B010/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ); `ع ظ م`: 69:52 (عَظِيمِ)
- Synthesis: Intrinsic nobility and a cherished trust contrast with competitive claims to generosity, rivalry in elevation, public good name, self-aggrandizement, and social dignity. The messenger's honor is relational and custodial rather than self-created.

### 39. P3: Poetry and divination are rival production systems
- Semantic invariant: Poetic composition and divinatory report have their own materials, marks, meters, and social agents, but they do not explain the message's source.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:41-42 explicitly denies that the speech belongs to a poet or soothsayer.
- Surprising reach: Poetry's root reaches hair, vegetation, barley, undergarment, blood-mark, star-name, and segmented meter; divination reaches hidden news, service, and tribal title.

#### Subchannel A. Metered poem, poet, and segmented line
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A craftsperson composes measured language whose units can be divided and compared.
- Active motifs: قريض وشاعر (`quranic:root_000798:B007/m01`); كلام وشعر مقطع (`quranic:root_001240:B022/m01`); إخراج القول بالنطق (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); كثرة القول في صاحبه (`quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`); تجزئة الشيء وطائفته (`quranic:root_000133:B001/m01`); إفراز النصيب وتقسيم الشيء (`quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `ب ع ض`: 69:44 (بَعْضَ); `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ)
- Synthesis: Poetry, poet, segmented verse, clipped speech, utterance, fluent speaker, part, and division describe a made verbal object. The denial identifies a genuine production system but excludes it as the source of this saying.

#### Subchannel B. Fine awareness, sign, and bodily slogan
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Subtle perception is externalized as a sign worn close to body or displayed in ritual and conflict.
- Active motifs: شعار يلاصق الجسد (`quranic:root_000798:B004/m01`); علم دقيق وفطنة (`quranic:root_000798:B005/m01`); علامة مشعرة وشعيرة نسك (`quranic:root_000798:B006/m01`); أثر يميز الشيء ويهدي إليه (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`); جريان الذكر على اللسان (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Undergarment or inward slogan, fine awareness, ritual or battle mark, guiding sign, and spoken mention move between inner apprehension and public emblem. The scene shows why poetic and prophetic language can be confused: both make the barely perceived socially visible.

#### Subchannel C. Hair, dense vegetation, and grain-scale analogy
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Fine repeated growth supplies material analogies for texture, density, and small units.
- Active motifs: الشَّعر النابت وما عليه زغب (`quranic:root_000798:B001/m01`); نبات كثيف كالشَّعر (`quranic:root_000798:B002/m01`); حبة الشعير وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000798:B003/m01`); جودة النبات والغيث (`quranic:root_001294:B002/m01`); العنب والكرمة (`quranic:root_001294:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ)
- Synthesis: Hair and down, hair-like dense vegetation, barley grain, fertile rain-bearing land, and grapevine create a scale from filament to field. Poetic vocabulary is grounded in inspectable natural forms rather than free-floating ornament.

#### Subchannel D. Soothsayer, hidden information, and assigned service
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A specialist claims access to concealed reports and may act as an attendant within a social group.
- Active motifs: الكاهن المتكهن بالأخبار الخفية (`quranic:root_001327:B001/m01`); الكاهن القائم بأمر الرجل (`quranic:root_001327:B002/m01`); الكاهنان لقب لقبيلتين (`quranic:root_001327:B003/m01`); انكشاف الشيء للعارف (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); أثر يميز الشيء ويهدي إليه (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`); أسماء مخصوصة منقولة (`quranic:root_000798:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ه ن`: 69:42 (كَاهِنٍ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ)
- Synthesis: Hidden-news diviner, appointed attendant, tribal title, knowledge, distinguishing mark, and transferred proper names define the social institution of soothsaying. The denial separates revelation from both speculative access and inherited office.

#### Subchannel E. Particle, definition, and formal language
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Speech is organized by small grammatical operators and explicit delimitation of meaning.
- Active motifs: حرف رب وربما (`quranic:root_000532:B015/m01`); قول الشيء حده (`quranic:root_001272:B016/m01`); الاسم تنويه ودلالة (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ); `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ)
- Synthesis: The particle `rubba`, logical definition, and naming form a formal language layer beneath extended utterance. These devices organize reference and scope without themselves identifying the message's source.

### 40. P3: Fabrication is exposed as attributed and circulating speech
- Semantic invariant: False discourse is produced by inventing words, assigning them to another, circulating them socially, and resisting the marks that disclose their source.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:44-47 imagines fabricated sayings attributed to God, and 69:49 names those who deny.
- Surprising reach: The lie reaches a failed military charge, stopping animal, vanished milk, painted cloth, rumor, logical definition, and the act of cutting speech into misleading fragments.

#### Subchannel A. Invented saying and false attribution
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A speaker composes what was not said and attaches it to an authority.
- Active motifs: قول ما لم يكن أو نسبته (`quranic:root_001272:B005/m01`); اجترار القول إلى النفس (`quranic:root_001272:B006/m01`); خلاف الصدق (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); نسبة الشيء أو صاحبه إلى الكذب (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); كلام وشعر مقطع (`quranic:root_001240:B022/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `ك ذ ب`: 69:49 (مُّكَذِّبِينَ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ)
- Synthesis: Fabrication, appropriation of a saying, lie, accusation of lying, and clipped or segmented speech define authorship fraud. The counterfactual is precise because both production and attribution must occur for the offense to exist.

#### Subchannel B. Circulating report, name, and public reputation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Speech detaches from its first speaker and becomes a social current of praise, blame, or rumor.
- Active motifs: كثرة القول في صاحبه (`quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`); القول الفاشي بين الناس (`quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`); المقاولة في الأمر (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`); جريان الذكر على اللسان (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`); ذكر المرء شرف وصيت (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`); الاسم تنويه ودلالة (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`); الصيت الحسن المنتشر (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`); هدية تطلب المكافأة (`quranic:root_001294:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ)
- Synthesis: Fluent speaker, circulating talk, negotiation, mention, renown, name, favorable reputation, and gift seeking reward through praise form a publicity system. False attribution gains force only if this circulation carries it.

#### Subchannel C. Failed continuation as embodied untruth
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An action's course contradicts its apparent promise by halting, disappearing, or failing under pressure.
- Active motifs: صدق الحملة أو كذبها (`quranic:root_001290:B004/m01`); ما كذب أن فعل أي ما لبث (`quranic:root_001290:B005/m01`); كذب لبن الناقة إذا ذهب ولم يدم (`quranic:root_001290:B006/m01`); كذب الوحشي إذا جرى ثم وقف (`quranic:root_001290:B007/m01`); عجز وانقطاع عن فعل (`quranic:root_001240:B020/m01`); الحَسير منقطع القوة (`quranic:root_000320:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ذ ب`: 69:49 (مُّكَذِّبِينَ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ)
- Synthesis: A charge that falters, action without delay, milk that vanishes, an animal that runs then stops, inability to continue, and exhausted power make 'lying' visible as a failed trajectory. The body and event expose what the initial appearance falsely promised.

#### Subchannel D. Deceptive surface and resistant inner self
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An outer pattern or covering misstates what lies beneath while the self sustains the deception.
- Active motifs: النفس الكذوب (`quranic:root_001290:B008/m01`); الكذابة ثوب يكذب بحاله (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); ستر وتغطية (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); حجب الحق (`quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`); غلظ الحافة ووصل الشقتين (`quranic:root_000121:B006/m01`); قول في النفس لم يظهر (`quranic:root_001272:B012/m01`); القول اعتقاد ومذهب (`quranic:root_001272:B013/m01`); قول الشيء حده (`quranic:root_001272:B016/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ذ ب`: 69:49 (مُّكَذِّبِينَ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `ب ص ر`: 69:38 (تُبْصِرُ), 69:39 (تُبْصِرُ); `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ)
- Synthesis: Deceptive self, multicolored cloth, covering, covered truth, stitched surface, inward statement, adopted doctrine, and formal definition distinguish outer sign from inner commitment. Exposure requires reading both layers.

### 41. P3: Seizure culminates in severing the lifeline
- Semantic invariant: Punitive interception moves from taking by superior force to cutting the vital conduit and foreclosing every intervention.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:45-47 describes taking by the right, cutting the aorta, and the inability of anyone to block the act.
- Surprising reach: The vital mechanism expands into grip, straight thrust, choking rope, abdominal rupture, severed hope, and the persistent vessel whose life ends when its fixed channel is cut.

#### Subchannel A. Taking by force and arresting grip
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A superior agent seizes the body at a usable hold and prevents counteraction.
- Active motifs: حوز الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`); المؤاخذة بالذنب (`quranic:root_000018:B002/m01`); القبض والأسر (`quranic:root_000018:B003/m01`); أخذة المصارعة (`quranic:root_000018:B011/m01`); مقبض الشيء المأخوذ به (`quranic:root_000018:B012/m01`); اليد اليمنى والجهة اليمنى (`quranic:root_001698:B002/m01`); يمين القوة والحق (`quranic:root_001698:B004/m01`); آلة قاطعة وحد أو نصل (`quranic:root_001240:B014/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء خ ذ`: 69:45 (أَخَذْ); `ي م ن`: 69:45 (يَمِينِ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ)
- Synthesis: Taking, accountability, capture, wrestling hold, handle, right hand, effective force, and cutting implement form the initial interception. Power is not abstract: it reaches the body through a grip and an edge.

#### Subchannel B. Aorta, fixed life-channel, and fatal cut
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A persistent conduit supplies the body until a precise cut terminates life.
- Active motifs: ثبات وملازمة (`quranic:root_001622:B001/m01`); الوتين عرق الحياة (`quranic:root_001622:B002/m01`); إصابة الوتين (`quranic:root_001622:B003/m01`); غلظ وتين الإبل (`quranic:root_001622:B005/m01`); الصَّرْم والإبانة (`quranic:root_001240:B001/m01`); انقطاع وذهاب ونهاية (`quranic:root_001240:B005/m01`); فصل أمر أو حق (`quranic:root_001240:B010/m01`); طعنة استقامت حتى نفذت (`quranic:root_000347:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `و ت ن`: 69:46 (وَتِينَ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ح ق ق`: 69:51 (حَقُّ)
- Synthesis: Persistence, the life-artery, injury to it, its bodily thickening, severance, termination, decisive separation of a matter, and a straight penetrating thrust define a targeted fatal mechanism. Cutting the conduit abolishes the continuity that had sustained the whole body.

#### Subchannel C. Choking, inner rupture, and exhausted breath
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Life is also stopped by constricting the airway or producing catastrophic internal interruption.
- Active motifs: اختناق وقطع النفس (`quranic:root_001240:B009/m01`); مغص وبهر وانقطاع باطن (`quranic:root_001240:B021/m01`); انقباض الأمعاء من الظمأ (`quranic:root_000297:B008/m01`); الحَسير منقطع القوة (`quranic:root_000320:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Noose and stopped breath, abdominal pain and panting, thirst-contracted intestines, and exhausted capacity show several bodily routes to the same terminal outcome. The surface selects the aorta, but the lexical field maps the wider physiology of interruption.

#### Subchannel D. No person, barrier, or kin-group can intervene
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Every possible intermediary is excluded from standing between agent and target.
- Active motifs: الأَحَدِيَّة والوَحْدَة (`quranic:root_000017:B001/m01`); استغراق النفي (`quranic:root_000017:B002/m01`); الانفراد والتفرق آحادا (`quranic:root_000017:B005/m01`); الحيلولة بفاصل (`quranic:root_000297:B001/m01`); إيقاف القتال بالمحاجزة (`quranic:root_000297:B002/m01`); حجز الرجل أصله وعشيرته (`quranic:root_000297:B006/m01`); انقطاع الرجاء والسفر (`quranic:root_001240:B006/m01`); هجر وصريمة وقطع وصل (`quranic:root_001240:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ); `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ)
- Synthesis: One person, anyone under total negation, separated individuals, physical barrier, fight-stopper, clan refuge, stranded traveler, and broken social bond enumerate possible interposers. None can convert the seizure into truce, rescue, or renewed connection.

### 42. P3: Protection can cover, gird, or fail
- Semantic invariant: Defense places a material or social layer between vulnerability and harm, but that layer can be stripped, bypassed, or become a restraint itself.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:47 denies any effective barrier, while 69:48 addresses those who actively guard themselves.
- Surprising reach: Protection reaches shield, armor, belt, animal tether, garment, seed-cover, clan, chastity, cautious hoof, and a crown that covers the ruler.

#### Subchannel A. Shield, armor, and guarded body
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A protective layer intercepts injury before it reaches flesh.
- Active motifs: دفع الضرر بوقاية (`quranic:root_001677:B001/m01`); جعل النفس في وقاية (`quranic:root_001677:B002/m01`); بصيرة السلاح (`quranic:root_000121:B005/m01`); الحاسر عار من الوقاية (`quranic:root_000320:B002/m01`); ستر وتغطية (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); تاج يغطي (`quranic:root_001307:B015/m01`); شعار يلاصق الجسد (`quranic:root_000798:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `و ق ي`: 69:48 (مُتَّقِينَ); `ب ص ر`: 69:38 (تُبْصِرُ), 69:39 (تُبْصِرُ); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ)
- Synthesis: Protection, self-guarding, shield, unarmored exposure, covering, crown, and body-clinging garment define defense at the skin boundary. The bare fighter reveals by contrast what the protective layer normally does.

#### Subchannel B. Belt, tether, and fitted restraint
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A binding member secures clothing, load, or animal body by joining it to a stable point.
- Active motifs: شد الوسط وحجزة اللباس (`quranic:root_000297:B004/m01`); رباط البعير المحجوز (`quranic:root_000297:B005/m01`); دفع الضرر بوقاية (`quranic:root_001677:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `و ق ي`: 69:48 (مُتَّقِينَ)
- Synthesis: Waistband, camel tether, and protective barrier share fastening mechanics. Function distinguishes aid from coercion: the same fitted restraint can keep a load safe or prevent a body from moving.

#### Subchannel C. Clan refuge, chastity, and social guarding
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person is protected by lineage, reputation, and controlled bodily boundaries.
- Active motifs: حجز الرجل أصله وعشيرته (`quranic:root_000297:B006/m01`); الحجزة كناية عن العفة (`quranic:root_000297:B007/m01`); موضع منقطع (`quranic:root_001307:B012/m01`); ثنية مستورة (`quranic:root_001307:B013/m01`); الانفراد والتفرق آحادا (`quranic:root_000017:B005/m01`); جعل النفس في وقاية (`quranic:root_001677:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ); `و ق ي`: 69:48 (مُتَّقِينَ)
- Synthesis: Clan origin, chastity, remote protected settlement, hidden mountain pass, individual separation, and self-guarding move defense from armor to social geography and conduct. Refuge works by controlling access.

#### Subchannel D. Cautious footing, measured weight, and warning bird
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A vulnerable traveler avoids injury by testing surface, load, and environmental sign.
- Active motifs: توقي الدابة من وجع الحافر (`quranic:root_001677:B003/m01`); الأوقية وزن معلوم (`quranic:root_001677:B004/m01`); الواقي اسم للصرد (`quranic:root_001677:B005/m01`); حجارة بصرة رخوة (`quranic:root_000121:B007/m01`); عبور مسافة أو ماء (`quranic:root_001240:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `و ق ي`: 69:48 (مُتَّقِينَ); `ب ص ر`: 69:38 (تُبْصِرُ), 69:39 (تُبْصِرُ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ)
- Synthesis: A horse protecting a sore hoof, a known weight, a warning bird, soft pale stones, and a water crossing form a practical risk scene. Guarding is anticipatory perception translated into careful motion.

### 43. P3: Reminder makes an inward trace publicly legible
- Semantic invariant: Remembering preserves content in the heart, reactivates it through speech or sign, and leaves a durable mark that can guide later recognition.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:42 contrasts little remembrance with soothsaying, and 69:48 calls the message a reminder for the self-guarding.
- Surprising reach: The reminder becomes a legal deed, male hardness, honor, battle-sign, road-marker, inscribed edge, prayer bead, and named star or mountain.

#### Subchannel A. Retention, recollection, and active prompting
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Stored content is brought back into attention and offered as a prompt to conduct.
- Active motifs: استحضار الشيء بعد النسيان أو مع الحفظ (`quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`); الذكرى والتذكرة ما يذكّر (`quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`); انكشاف الشيء للعارف (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); تصديق يطمئن إليه القلب (`quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`); جعل النفس في وقاية (`quranic:root_001677:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `ء م ن`: 69:41 (تُؤْمِنُ); `و ق ي`: 69:48 (مُتَّقِينَ)
- Synthesis: Retention against forgetting, reminder, knowledge, assent, and self-guarding form the cognitive-behavioral sequence. The reminder succeeds when recovered content becomes anticipatory protection.

#### Subchannel B. Spoken mention, honor, and durable deed
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Memory is externalized as repeated name, public reputation, or written evidence of a right.
- Active motifs: جريان الذكر على اللسان (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`); ذكر المرء شرف وصيت (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`); ذكر الحق صك ووثيقة حق (`quranic:root_000516:B008/m01`); الاسم تنويه ودلالة (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`); الصيت الحسن المنتشر (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`); أثر يميز الشيء ويهدي إليه (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ)
- Synthesis: Mention, renown, deed of right, name, favorable reputation, and identifying mark carry memory outside an individual mind. They let later observers recognize a person, claim, or path.

#### Subchannel C. Ritual sign, bodily mark, and counted remembrance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A visible or tactile token cues communal identity and repeated devotional action.
- Active motifs: علامة مشعرة وشعيرة نسك (`quranic:root_000798:B006/m01`); أثر يميز الشيء ويهدي إليه (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`); شق ظاهر في الشفة العليا (`quranic:root_001040:B004/m01`); خرز التسبيح (`quranic:root_000666:B006/m01`); الذكر خلاف الأنثى (`quranic:root_000516:B001/m01`); صلابة الذكر وحدته وشدته (`quranic:root_000516:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `س ب ح`: 69:52 (سَبِّحْ); `ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Ritual or battle sign, guiding mark, split lip, counting beads, male classification, and hard masculine material show remembrance embodied in tokens. Mark and repetition keep identity available to eye, hand, and community.

#### Subchannel D. Proper name and transferred location
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A lexical form preserves identity by becoming the stable name of person, star, mountain, or place.
- Active motifs: أسماء مخصوصة منقولة (`quranic:root_000798:B008/m01`); الأول والإضافة (`quranic:root_000017:B004/m01`); جبل أُحُد (`quranic:root_000017:B006/m01`); العزيز الذي يكرم عليك (`quranic:root_001294:B010/m01`); الاسم تنويه ودلالة (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ)
- Synthesis: Transferred star, mountain, clan, and personal names; Sunday as the first day; Mount Uhud; cherished person; and name itself turn memory into a fixed index. A proper name is a compact reminder whose referent must still be correctly identified.

### 44. P3: Covering truth matures into exposure and regret
- Semantic invariant: Concealment initially blocks recognition, but removal of the cover leaves the denier exposed, exhausted, and grieving what cannot be recovered.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:49-50 joins knowledge of deniers to the message becoming regret for them.
- Surprising reach: The process reaches cloud clearing, receding sea, bare armorless body, molting feathers, depleted gaze, remote grave, covered seed, and expiation that attempts to cover fault differently.

#### Subchannel A. Cover, darkness, and obscured truth
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A layer hides object, light, gratitude, or acknowledged reality from view.
- Active motifs: ستر وتغطية (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); غمر ساتر (`quranic:root_001307:B002/m01`); حجب الحق (`quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`); ستر النعمة (`quranic:root_001307:B004/m01`); الحَسْر كشف الغطاء وإزالة الساتر (`quranic:root_000320:B001/m01`); إبصار العين (`quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`); بصيرة القلب (`quranic:root_000121:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `ب ص ر`: 69:38 (تُبْصِرُ), 69:39 (تُبْصِرُ)
- Synthesis: Covering, darkness or engulfing water, concealment of truth, concealment of benefit, and the act of stripping a cover frame the conflict between visibility and denial. Eye and insight specify what the cover prevents.

#### Subchannel B. Uncovering, armorless exposure, and exhausted perception
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Protective or obscuring layers recede until body and sight stand without reserve.
- Active motifs: الحَسْر كشف الغطاء وإزالة الساتر (`quranic:root_000320:B001/m01`); الحاسر عار من الوقاية (`quranic:root_000320:B002/m01`); الحَسير منقطع القوة (`quranic:root_000320:B003/m01`); ستر وتغطية (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); دفع الضرر بوقاية (`quranic:root_001677:B001/m01`); إبصار العين (`quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `و ق ي`: 69:48 (مُتَّقِينَ); `ب ص ر`: 69:38 (تُبْصِرُ), 69:39 (تُبْصِرُ)
- Synthesis: Cloud or sea is swept aside, the fighter is left without armor, vision and strength run out, and the ordinary cover is gone. Protection is conceptually present only as what failed to remain between subject and consequence.

#### Subchannel C. Molting, bodily reduction, and social exclusion
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Loss of covering appears as shed hair or feather, reduced flesh, and exclusion from protected spaces.
- Active motifs: التحسر سقوط الريش والشعر وانضواء اللحم (`quranic:root_000320:B005/m01`); المُحَسَّر محقر مقصى (`quranic:root_000320:B006/m01`); موضع منقطع (`quranic:root_001307:B012/m01`); الشَّعر النابت وما عليه زغب (`quranic:root_000798:B001/m01`); انقطاع الرجاء والسفر (`quranic:root_001240:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ)
- Synthesis: Molting feather, lost hair, tightened flesh, banishment from royal doors, remote settlement or grave, body hair, and stranded traveler make exposure both physiological and social. What is lost is a layer of belonging as much as a physical covering.

#### Subchannel D. Regret for the lost and attempted covering of fault
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The exposed person grieves an irrecoverable loss while lexical expiation imagines fault being covered in another, reparative mode.
- Active motifs: الحَسرة تلهف على فائت (`quranic:root_000320:B004/m01`); تبرؤ وتنصل (`quranic:root_001307:B005/m01`); نسبة إلى الكفر (`quranic:root_001307:B006/m01`); إلجاء إلى العصيان (`quranic:root_001307:B007/m01`); محو الإثم بتغطيته (`quranic:root_001307:B009/m01`); خضوع متطامن (`quranic:root_001307:B014/m01`); نسبة الشيء أو صاحبه إلى الكذب (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `ك ذ ب`: 69:49 (مُّكَذِّبِينَ)
- Synthesis: Regret, disavowal, accusation of unbelief, coercion into disobedience, expiation, submissive bowing, and accusation of lying map responses after fault becomes visible. Covering truth and covering sin are opposite operations: one prevents acknowledgment, the other seeks accountable repair.

### 45. P3: Sacred elevation resolves into naming and praise
- Semantic invariant: Divine transcendence is articulated through lordship, greatness, elevated name, purification from defect, worship, and grateful movement.
- Surface relation: direct; 69:43 names the Lord as source, and 69:52 commands glorification through the name of the great Lord.
- Surprising reach: Praise reaches prayer, voluntary worship, counted beads, running and swimming, strong leather garments, holy locations, cloud-canopy, and the leader of sailors.

#### Subchannel A. Lordship, cultivation, and sacred knowledge
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Sovereignty is joined to the patient work of sustaining, teaching, and ordering what is under care.
- Active motifs: ربوبية وملك وسيادة (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); إصلاح وتربية وإتمام (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); علم رباني (`quranic:root_000532:B003/m01`); ربابة عهد وميثاق (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`); رباني الملاحين (`quranic:root_000532:B017/m01`); انكشاف الشيء للعارف (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); الكبر والقوة (`quranic:root_001029:B001/m01`); الحرمة والشرف (`quranic:root_001029:B010/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `ع ظ م`: 69:52 (عَظِيمِ)
- Synthesis: Lordship, gradual cultivation, learned devotion, covenant, captaincy, knowledge, greatness, and sacred dignity frame authority as both superior and sustaining. The name in the final command belongs to a relation of care, not merely force.

#### Subchannel B. Greatness, awe, and rejection of self-magnification
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: True magnitude evokes reverence, while human self-enlargement distorts scale.
- Active motifs: الكبر والقوة (`quranic:root_001029:B001/m01`); معظم الشيء (`quranic:root_001029:B002/m01`); العظيمة النازلة (`quranic:root_001029:B004/m01`); التعاظم والزهو (`quranic:root_001029:B006/m01`); الاستعظام والهيبة (`quranic:root_001029:B007/m01`); الحرمة والشرف (`quranic:root_001029:B010/m01`); العلو والارتفاع (`quranic:root_000745:B001/m01`); الشخص المرتفع الظاهر (`quranic:root_000745:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع ظ م`: 69:52 (عَظِيمِ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ)
- Synthesis: Magnitude, major portion, overwhelming calamity, arrogance, awe, honor, height, and visible elevated figure distinguish objective greatness from performed self-aggrandizement. Praise restores the scale that pride falsifies.

#### Subchannel C. Name, elevated reputation, and incomparable rank
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Naming makes exalted identity present in speech without reducing it to a visible body.
- Active motifs: الاسم تنويه ودلالة (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`); المساماة والمباراة (`quranic:root_000745:B007/m01`); الصيت الحسن المنتشر (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`); الشرف والجود المحمود (`quranic:root_001294:B001/m01`); العزيز الذي يكرم عليك (`quranic:root_001294:B010/m01`); انفراد بلا نظير (`quranic:root_001240:B018/m01`); الأَحَدِيَّة والوَحْدَة (`quranic:root_000017:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ)
- Synthesis: Name, rivalry in elevation, good renown, nobility, cherished uniqueness, unmatched rank, and oneness establish identity through distinction. The final naming act is praise precisely because the referent exceeds competitors and substitutes.

#### Subchannel D. Purification, worship, and expansive motion
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Praise clears defect from the named one and releases the worshipper into repeated devotional movement.
- Active motifs: العبادة بالتسبيح والصلاة (`quranic:root_000666:B001/m01`); التنزيه والتبرئة (`quranic:root_000666:B002/m01`); السبح في الجري والعوم (`quranic:root_000666:B004/m01`); السعة للذهاب والمعاش (`quranic:root_000666:B005/m01`); خرز التسبيح (`quranic:root_000666:B006/m01`); سباح الجلود والكساء (`quranic:root_000666:B007/m01`); سبوحة الموضع (`quranic:root_000666:B008/m01`); جريان الذكر على اللسان (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ب ح`: 69:52 (سَبِّحْ); `ذ ك ر`: 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Prayer, purification from defect, swimming or running, freedom to move for livelihood, counting beads, strong ritual clothing, sacred place, and spoken mention turn glorification into embodied practice. Motion and material token prevent praise from remaining an unperformed abstraction.

### 46. P3: Quantity is formed by part, share, one, and few
- Semantic invariant: A whole is analyzed through partition, allotted share, discrete unit, small amount, and singular identity.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:41-42 repeats 'little,' 69:44 imagines some sayings, and 69:47 negates every single intervenor.
- Surprising reach: Quantification reaches water allocation, blood-oath distribution, large jars, mountain peaks, cloth-folding, tremor, Sunday, and isolated individuals.

#### Subchannel A. Part, severed piece, and distributed share
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A whole is divided into portions that can be assigned, carried, or judged separately.
- Active motifs: تجزئة الشيء وطائفته (`quranic:root_000133:B001/m01`); إفراز النصيب وتقسيم الشيء (`quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`); قطعة مفصولة وطائفة (`quranic:root_001240:B002/m01`); تفريق وتقسيم وتجزئة (`quranic:root_001240:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ب ع ض`: 69:44 (بَعْضَ); `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ)
- Synthesis: Part, allotted share, separated piece, and division into groups or kinds establish partition as both conceptual and material. The imagined 'some sayings' gains precision as a portion detached from a larger discourse.

#### Subchannel B. One, anyone, individual, and first position
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Singularity can identify a unique unit, exhaust a negated class, or isolate individuals from a group.
- Active motifs: الأَحَدِيَّة والوَحْدَة (`quranic:root_000017:B001/m01`); استغراق النفي (`quranic:root_000017:B002/m01`); الواحد في العد والتركيب (`quranic:root_000017:B003/m01`); الأول والإضافة (`quranic:root_000017:B004/m01`); الانفراد والتفرق آحادا (`quranic:root_000017:B005/m01`); انفراد بلا نظير (`quranic:root_001240:B018/m01`); ثبات وملازمة (`quranic:root_001622:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `و ت ن`: 69:46 (وَتِينَ)
- Synthesis: Unity, all-inclusive anyone, numerical one, first day, separated individuals, unmatched singularity, and persistence distinguish several roles for the unit. In the surface negation, one does not mean a privileged exception but exhaustive failure of every possible person.

#### Subchannel C. Fewness, summit, jar, load, and tremor
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A root of small quantity unfolds into upper point, containing vessel, lifted burden, and a body's slight but visible disturbance.
- Active motifs: القِلَّة والضآلة (`quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`); قُلَّة الشيء ورأسه (`quranic:root_001251:B002/m01`); القُلَّة الجرة الكبيرة (`quranic:root_001251:B003/m01`); الإقلال والاستقلال حملا ونهوضا (`quranic:root_001251:B004/m01`); القِلُّ رعدة واضطراب (`quranic:root_001251:B005/m01`); حسن موزع في الوجه (`quranic:root_001226:B001/m01`); طي القسامي أول الثوب (`quranic:root_001226:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ل ل`: 69:41 (قَلِيلًا), 69:42 (قَلِيلًا); `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ)
- Synthesis: Fewness, peak, large jar, carrying and departure, tremor, well-proportioned face, and first fold of cloth show quantity through scale, elevation, containment, motion, and form. The repeated 'little' sits within this broader calibration of how much is present or activated.

#### Subchannel D. Oath distributed across persons and outcomes
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A collective claim is stabilized by assigning vows, shares, and responsibility among participants.
- Active motifs: يمين مقسومة على أهلها (`quranic:root_001226:B004/m01`); بال مقسم بين وجوه الأمر (`quranic:root_001226:B006/m01`); هدنة قسامة (`quranic:root_001226:B008/m01`); يمين الحلف (`quranic:root_001698:B003/m01`); المقاولة في الأمر (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ); `ي م ن`: 69:45 (يَمِينِ); `ق و ل`: 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ)
- Synthesis: Oath, divided deliberation, truce, sworn pledge, and negotiation form a social arithmetic. Speech is apportioned to responsible agents rather than left as an anonymous collective murmur.

### 47. P3: Conflict is interrupted, crossed, or concluded
- Semantic invariant: Opposed parties move from challenge and capture through boundary crossing to intervention, truce, or severed relation.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:45-47 supplies seizure, fatal force, and failed intervention as a compact conflict scene.
- Surprising reach: Conflict reaches war-slogan, blood oath, duel, highway interception, migration, racing, clan refuge, garment-belt grip, and a geographic region named for separation.

#### Subchannel A. Challenge, duel, and competitive superiority
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Opponents descend into direct contest and test strength, speed, or public excellence.
- Active motifs: النزول للمبارزة (`quranic:root_001492:B007/m01`); مفاخرة الكرم والغلبة فيه (`quranic:root_001294:B006/m01`); سبق وسرعة تترك وراءها (`quranic:root_001240:B017/m01`); انفراد بلا نظير (`quranic:root_001240:B018/m01`); أخذة المصارعة (`quranic:root_000018:B011/m01`); بال مقسم بين وجوه الأمر (`quranic:root_001226:B006/m01`); الشدة النازلة (`quranic:root_001492:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ن ز ل`: 69:43 (تَنزِيلٌ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:45 (أَخَذْ); `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ)
- Synthesis: Duel, descending calamity, rivalry in generosity, racing speed, unmatched capacity, wrestling hold, and tactical deliberation stage contest at bodily and reputational levels. The issue is who can carry action through rather than merely claim superiority.

#### Subchannel B. War sign, blood oath, and captured body
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Combatants identify their side, bind themselves by oath, and seize an opponent.
- Active motifs: علامة مشعرة وشعيرة نسك (`quranic:root_000798:B006/m01`); يمين مقسومة على أهلها (`quranic:root_001226:B004/m01`); القبض والأسر (`quranic:root_000018:B003/m01`); أخذة المصارعة (`quranic:root_000018:B011/m01`); يمين القوة والحق (`quranic:root_001698:B004/m01`); الحاسر عار من الوقاية (`quranic:root_000320:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ); `ء خ ذ`: 69:45 (أَخَذْ); `ي م ن`: 69:45 (يَمِينِ); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Battle-sign, blood-oath, capture, wrestling grip, force, and armorless fighter form a close combat frame. Signs and vows organize collective violence before the grip individualizes it on a body.

#### Subchannel C. Road interception, crossing, and stranded traveler
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Movement through a route is opposed, diverted, or broken by hostile control.
- Active motifs: عبور مسافة أو ماء (`quranic:root_001240:B003/m01`); ذهاب الطير ورجوعه بين البلاد (`quranic:root_001240:B004/m01`); انقطاع الرجاء والسفر (`quranic:root_001240:B006/m01`); قطع الطريق بالغصب والصد (`quranic:root_001240:B023/m01`); الحجاز الفاصل بين البلاد (`quranic:root_000297:B003/m01`); ثنية مستورة (`quranic:root_001307:B013/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ)
- Synthesis: Water or road crossing, seasonal bird movement, stranded traveler, highway robbery, the separating Hijaz, and hidden mountain passage map conflict onto mobility. Control of route can isolate without direct bodily combat.

#### Subchannel D. Interposition, truce, and broken relation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A third force may separate fighters and preserve peace, or separation may harden into abandonment.
- Active motifs: الحيلولة بفاصل (`quranic:root_000297:B001/m01`); إيقاف القتال بالمحاجزة (`quranic:root_000297:B002/m01`); هدنة قسامة (`quranic:root_001226:B008/m01`); هجر وصريمة وقطع وصل (`quranic:root_001240:B007/m01`); تبرؤ وتنصل (`quranic:root_001307:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ)
- Synthesis: Barrier, separation of combatants, truce, severed relation, and disavowal distinguish protective interruption from hostile rupture. The surface denial says no such mediator can halt the divine action.

### 48. P3: Garment construction makes covering inspectable
- Semantic invariant: Textile is produced through folding, stitching, cutting, fitting, fastening, and covering a body or vessel.
- Surface relation: indirect; the visible/invisible oath and the stripping of every possible defense make surfaces and coverings semantically active.
- Surprising reach: The craft reaches hide sewn to hide, first fold, tailored pieces, saddle-cloth, waistband, necklace, vessel lid, leather children's clothes, and royal crown.

#### Subchannel A. Fold, seam, and joined hide
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Flat material is folded and stitched so separate edges become one usable surface.
- Active motifs: طي القسامي أول الثوب (`quranic:root_001226:B007/m01`); غلظ الحافة ووصل الشقتين (`quranic:root_000121:B006/m01`); شعار يلاصق الجسد (`quranic:root_000798:B004/m01`); تفصيل ثوب ومقطعات (`quranic:root_001240:B012/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ); `ب ص ر`: 69:38 (تُبْصِرُ), 69:39 (تُبْصِرُ); `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ)
- Synthesis: First fold, stitched hide, body-clinging undergarment, and tailored cloth define construction from initial lay to joined surface. The seam is visible evidence of how the covering was made.

#### Subchannel B. Cut garment, saddle layer, and fitted bodily form
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Material is cut into pieces whose shape corresponds to body or load.
- Active motifs: تفصيل ثوب ومقطعات (`quranic:root_001240:B012/m01`); سوط وطاء مصنوعان من قطع (`quranic:root_001240:B015/m01`); شبه وقد وهيئة ممشوقة (`quranic:root_001240:B019/m01`); التحسر سقوط الريش والشعر وانضواء اللحم (`quranic:root_000320:B005/m01`); رأس الفخذ المستدير (`quranic:root_001294:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ)
- Synthesis: Tailored garment, segmented whip or saddle-cloth, proportioned bodily shape, reduced flesh after shedding, and the rounded hip-joint move between fabric pattern and anatomical fit. Cutting can produce a usable form rather than simply destroy.

#### Subchannel C. Waist fastening, armor cover, and protective clothing
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A garment is secured at the body's middle and layered over vulnerable surfaces or equipment.
