- Active motifs: شد الوسط وحجزة اللباس (`quranic:root_000297:B004/m01`); ستر وتغطية (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); تاج يغطي (`quranic:root_001307:B015/m01`); دفع الضرر بوقاية (`quranic:root_001677:B001/m01`); الحاسر عار من الوقاية (`quranic:root_000320:B002/m01`); سباح الجلود والكساء (`quranic:root_000666:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `و ق ي`: 69:48 (مُتَّقِينَ); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `س ب ح`: 69:52 (سَبِّحْ)
- Synthesis: Waistband, cloth over armor, crown, protective barrier, exposed unarmored body, and strong leather clothing show covering as fitted defense and status. Fastening determines whether the layer remains functional under motion.

#### Subchannel D. Neck ornament, vessel lid, and ceremonial finish
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A finished object receives an upper closure or ornament that signals completion and rank.
- Active motifs: الكَرْم المنظوم في العنق (`quranic:root_001294:B003/m01`); طبق على رأس الوعاء (`quranic:root_001294:B005/m01`); تاج يغطي (`quranic:root_001307:B015/m01`); الشخص المرتفع الظاهر (`quranic:root_000745:B002/m01`); الحرمة والشرف (`quranic:root_001029:B010/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ); `ع ظ م`: 69:52 (عَظِيمِ)
- Synthesis: Necklace, lid over jar or pot, crown, elevated visible figure, and dignity place finish at the upper boundary. Closure and ornament both announce that the object or wearer has been deliberately completed.

### 49. P3: Water descends, gathers, and is crossed
- Semantic invariant: The water cycle runs from cloud and descent to gathered mass, allocated share, travel across it, and receding exposure of what lay beneath.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:38-39's visible and invisible range and 69:43's descent activate material images of rain, sea, and crossing.
- Surprising reach: Hydrology reaches lordly cloud, great well, oceanic darkness, apportioned irrigation, swimming, bird migration, and a route that ends when water or provisions fail.

#### Subchannel A. Cloud, sky, and released rain
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water gathers overhead and is sent down from an elevated cover.
- Active motifs: رباب السحاب (`quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`); السماء وما علا فأظل (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); هبوط الشيء وحلوله (`quranic:root_001492:B001/m01`); إنزال الشيء وإيصاله (`quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`); الإرسال والانبعاث (`quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`); جودة النبات والغيث (`quranic:root_001294:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ); `س م و`: 69:52 (ٱسْمِ); `ن ز ل`: 69:43 (تَنزِيلٌ); `ر س ل`: 69:40 (رَسُولٍ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ)
- Synthesis: Layered cloud, sky and rain, descent, caused descent, dispatch, and fertile rain-bearing ground form a complete precipitation scene. Sending and descending translate source authority into material delivery.

#### Subchannel B. Great water, well, and engulfing darkness
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water gathers at a scale that can sustain, conceal, or overwhelm.
- Active motifs: ماء كثير مجتمع في عيلم (`quranic:root_001040:B005/m01`); ماء رَبَب كثير (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`); غمر ساتر (`quranic:root_001307:B002/m01`); الحَسْر كشف الغطاء وإزالة الساتر (`quranic:root_000320:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ)
- Synthesis: Great sea or abundant well, plentiful water, oceanic or nocturnal covering, and sea receding from shore describe mass water through depth, concealment, and withdrawal. What becomes visible depends on the water's level.

#### Subchannel C. Allocated flow and water crossing
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A body of water is divided into usable shares or traversed along a controlled line.
- Active motifs: إفراز النصيب وتقسيم الشيء (`quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`); عبور مسافة أو ماء (`quranic:root_001240:B003/m01`); انقطاع وذهاب ونهاية (`quranic:root_001240:B005/m01`); السبح في الجري والعوم (`quranic:root_000666:B004/m01`); الأوقية وزن معلوم (`quranic:root_001677:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `س ب ح`: 69:52 (سَبِّحْ); `و ق ي`: 69:48 (مُتَّقِينَ)
- Synthesis: Apportioned water, river crossing, water's endpoint, swimming, and known weight make hydrology measurable. Share, route, termination, bodily motion, and load determine whether the water supports or obstructs passage.

#### Subchannel D. Roaming over land and water
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Living bodies move freely through a broad medium until a route or resource ends.
- Active motifs: السبح في الجري والعوم (`quranic:root_000666:B004/m01`); السعة للذهاب والمعاش (`quranic:root_000666:B005/m01`); ذهاب الطير ورجوعه بين البلاد (`quranic:root_001240:B004/m01`); انقطاع الرجاء والسفر (`quranic:root_001240:B006/m01`); سبق وسرعة تترك وراءها (`quranic:root_001240:B017/m01`); الانفراد والتفرق آحادا (`quranic:root_000017:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ب ح`: 69:52 (سَبِّحْ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ)
- Synthesis: Swimming, running, free movement for livelihood, bird migration, stranded travel, racing speed, and individual dispersal form a mobility field spanning water and land. Freedom is defined by available medium and sustained provision.

### 50. P3: Cultivation alternates cover, emergence, and harvest economy
- Semantic invariant: Agriculture covers seed, receives rain, raises plant and fruit, protects produce, and allocates land, flock, and labor.
- Surface relation: indirect; revelation's descent and the reminder's protective function resonate with rain, seed-cover, and guarded yield.
- Surprising reach: The field includes grapevine, barley, fruit sheath, camphor, pasture plant, mosquito, hawk, hyena, shrike, migratory birds, land grant, and wage contract.

#### Subchannel A. Covered seed and emerging crop
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The cultivator hides seed in soil so rain can convert concealment into visible growth.
- Active motifs: تغطية البذر (`quranic:root_001307:B008/m01`); جودة النبات والغيث (`quranic:root_001294:B002/m01`); العنب والكرمة (`quranic:root_001294:B004/m01`); نبات كثيف كالشَّعر (`quranic:root_000798:B002/m01`); حبة الشعير وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000798:B003/m01`); إنزال الشيء وإيصاله (`quranic:root_001492:B002/m01`); ربة نبات (`quranic:root_000532:B012/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ); `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ن ز ل`: 69:43 (تَنزِيلٌ); `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ)
- Synthesis: Farmer covering seed, fertile rain, vine, dense vegetation, barley grain, descending water, and persistent green plant form the sowing-to-growth sequence. Concealment is productive here because it prepares emergence rather than suppressing truth.

#### Subchannel B. Fruit sheath, perfume, and prepared yield
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Produce matures inside a cover and is transformed into valued food, scent, or guest provision.
- Active motifs: كمام الثمر (`quranic:root_001307:B010/m01`); كافور طيب (`quranic:root_001307:B011/m01`); النزل المعد للنازل (`quranic:root_001492:B005/m01`); العنب والكرمة (`quranic:root_001294:B004/m01`); هدية تطلب المكافأة (`quranic:root_001294:B008/m01`); رُبّ خاثر وإصلاح به (`quranic:root_000532:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `ن ز ل`: 69:43 (تَنزِيلٌ); `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ); `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ)
- Synthesis: Fruit sheath, camphor, prepared guest provision, grape, gift seeking return, and thick syrup or clarified residue trace produce from protected growth into refined social use.

#### Subchannel C. Land grant, flock, and contracted labor
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Agrarian production is organized through allocated land, herds, and agreed work.
- Active motifs: إقطاع وإذن واقتطاع (`quranic:root_001240:B013/m01`); قطيع من النعم والغنم (`quranic:root_001240:B016/m01`); مقاطعة على أجر أو عمل (`quranic:root_001240:B024/m01`); الحُسار نبات خاص (`quranic:root_000320:B007/m01`); السعة للذهاب والمعاش (`quranic:root_000666:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `س ب ح`: 69:52 (سَبِّحْ)
- Synthesis: Land allotment, flock, wage contract, pasture plant, and movement for livelihood define an agrarian economy. Property and labor are not background categories; they determine who can turn land into provision.

#### Subchannel D. Field pests, predators, and migratory animals
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Cultivated space is shared with small pests, warning birds, raptors, scavengers, and seasonal movement.
- Active motifs: البعوضة لصغرها وإيذائها (`quranic:root_000133:B002/m01`); طائر جارح يسمى العلام (`quranic:root_001040:B006/m01`); ذكر الضباع يسمى العيلام (`quranic:root_001040:B007/m01`); الواقي اسم للصرد (`quranic:root_001677:B005/m01`); ذهاب الطير ورجوعه بين البلاد (`quranic:root_001240:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ب ع ض`: 69:44 (بَعْضَ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ); `و ق ي`: 69:48 (مُتَّقِينَ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ)
- Synthesis: Mosquito, hawk, male hyena, shrike, and migratory birds make the field an ecological scene rather than a closed human asset. Visibility, warning, predation, and seasonal movement pressure the stability of yield.

### 51. P3: Perception and life fail through bodily depletion
- Semantic invariant: The body registers truth and consequence through eye, skin, hair, gut, breath, circulation, tremor, and fatigue.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:38-39 begins with seeing, and 69:45-46 ends in a precisely fatal bodily intervention.
- Surprising reach: Physiology reaches staring eye, lip split, hair loss, armorless skin, thirst-contracted intestine, high breath, colic, hip socket, semen, and the artery that feeds the liver.

#### Subchannel A. Eye, gaze, and exhausted vision
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Sight depends on an intact organ, directed attention, and sustained capacity.
- Active motifs: إبصار العين (`quranic:root_000121:B001/m01`); بصيرة القلب (`quranic:root_000121:B002/m01`); الحَسير منقطع القوة (`quranic:root_000320:B003/m01`); شق ظاهر في الشفة العليا (`quranic:root_001040:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ب ص ر`: 69:38 (تُبْصِرُ), 69:39 (تُبْصِرُ); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `ع ل م`: 69:43 (عَٰلَمِينَ), 69:49 (نَعْلَمُ)
- Synthesis: Eye-sight, inward sight, exhausted gaze, and split upper lip locate perception in vulnerable bodily structures. Seeing can fail by fatigue or injury even when truth remains present.

#### Subchannel B. Hair, skin, covering, and shedding
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The body's outer layer grows, protects, and is periodically or traumatically lost.
- Active motifs: الشَّعر النابت وما عليه زغب (`quranic:root_000798:B001/m01`); الحاسر عار من الوقاية (`quranic:root_000320:B002/m01`); التحسر سقوط الريش والشعر وانضواء اللحم (`quranic:root_000320:B005/m01`); ستر وتغطية (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); سباح الجلود والكساء (`quranic:root_000666:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `س ب ح`: 69:52 (سَبِّحْ)
- Synthesis: Hair and down, armorless exposure, molting or hair loss, covering, and strong leather clothing turn the body's surface into a changing protective medium.

#### Subchannel C. Gut contraction, colic, tremor, and high breath
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Stress appears as involuntary contraction, abdominal interruption, shaking, and respiratory strain.
- Active motifs: انقباض الأمعاء من الظمأ (`quranic:root_000297:B008/m01`); مغص وبهر وانقطاع باطن (`quranic:root_001240:B021/m01`); القِلُّ رعدة واضطراب (`quranic:root_001251:B005/m01`); الحَسير منقطع القوة (`quranic:root_000320:B003/m01`); حر الهاجرة (`quranic:root_001226:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ق ل ل`: 69:41 (قَلِيلًا), 69:42 (قَلِيلًا); `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ)
- Synthesis: Thirst-contracted intestine, colic and panting, tremor, spent capacity, and midday heat form an escalating failure of internal regulation under pressure.

#### Subchannel D. Joint, life-artery, seed, and bodily continuity
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Life persists through articulated support, circulating supply, and reproductive transmission.
- Active motifs: رأس الفخذ المستدير (`quranic:root_001294:B007/m01`); الوتين عرق الحياة (`quranic:root_001622:B002/m01`); غلظ وتين الإبل (`quranic:root_001622:B005/m01`); ماء الرجل الخارج (`quranic:root_001492:B009/m01`); شاة رُبّى وحداثة (`quranic:root_000532:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ك ر م`: 69:40 (كَرِيمٍ); `و ت ن`: 69:46 (وَتِينَ); `ن ز ل`: 69:43 (تَنزِيلٌ); `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ)
- Synthesis: Hip joint, life-artery, thickened vessel, reproductive fluid, and recent birth or youth connect mechanical articulation, circulation, and generation. The fatal cut attacks one conduit in a wider system of bodily continuity.

### 52. P3: Social distance is made spatial
- Semantic invariant: Exclusion, kinship, refuge, remoteness, and individual separation are expressed through geography and controlled access.
- Surface relation: indirect; 69:47's 'anyone among you' and 69:49's division within the audience activate the social field.
- Surprising reach: The field reaches clan-origin, Hijaz as separator, remote village or grave, mountain pass, banishment from court, stranded stranger, secluded woman, and holy valley.

#### Subchannel A. Clan origin and protected access
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Lineage and bodily reputation provide a social enclosure within which a person claims safety.
- Active motifs: حجز الرجل أصله وعشيرته (`quranic:root_000297:B006/m01`); الحجزة كناية عن العفة (`quranic:root_000297:B007/m01`); ربة وجماعات كثيرة (`quranic:root_000532:B004/m01`); ربابة عهد وميثاق (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`); رُبَى حاجة وعقدة ونعمة (`quranic:root_000532:B016/m01`); الأَحَدِيَّة والوَحْدَة (`quranic:root_000017:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `ر ب ب`: 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ); `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ)
- Synthesis: Clan root, chastity, gathered groups, covenant, tightly knotted need or favor, and singular person describe belonging through recognized origin and obligation. Protection depends on whether the social enclosure remains answerable to its member.

#### Subchannel B. Separating region, hidden pass, and remote settlement
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Distance and terrain regulate who can reach a community or place.
- Active motifs: الحجاز الفاصل بين البلاد (`quranic:root_000297:B003/m01`); موضع منقطع (`quranic:root_001307:B012/m01`); ثنية مستورة (`quranic:root_001307:B013/m01`); سبوحة الموضع (`quranic:root_000666:B008/m01`); جبل أُحُد (`quranic:root_000017:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `س ب ح`: 69:52 (سَبِّحْ); `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ)
- Synthesis: Hijaz as separator, remote village or grave, hidden mountain pass, sacred location, and Mount Uhud turn social distance into named geography. Place preserves identity while also limiting access.

#### Subchannel C. Banishment, strandedness, and severed relation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A person loses entry to patron, route, or kin and is left without a sustaining relation.
- Active motifs: المُحَسَّر محقر مقصى (`quranic:root_000320:B006/m01`); انقطاع الرجاء والسفر (`quranic:root_001240:B006/m01`); هجر وصريمة وقطع وصل (`quranic:root_001240:B007/m01`); تبرؤ وتنصل (`quranic:root_001307:B005/m01`); الانفراد والتفرق آحادا (`quranic:root_000017:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح س ر`: 69:50 (حَسْرَةٌ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ك ف ر`: 69:50 (كَٰفِرِينَ); `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ)
- Synthesis: Exclusion from royal doors, stranded traveler, severed relation, disavowal, and isolated individuals describe social death as loss of passage and sponsor.

#### Subchannel D. Secluded woman, guarded boundary, and controlled meeting
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Protection is achieved by regulating visibility and access to a person.
- Active motifs: صون الجارية وخدرها (`quranic:root_001696:B002/m01`); دفع الضرر بوقاية (`quranic:root_001677:B001/m01`); جعل النفس في وقاية (`quranic:root_001677:B002/m01`); الحجزة كناية عن العفة (`quranic:root_000297:B007/m01`); هدنة قسامة (`quranic:root_001226:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ي ق ن`: 69:51 (يَقِينِ); `و ق ي`: 69:48 (مُتَّقِينَ); `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ)
- Synthesis: Secluded protected woman, barrier, self-guarding, chastity, and truce define safety through controlled encounter. The scene is relational: it specifies who may approach and under what peace.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. Lot vessel, draw, and assigned share
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Lots are gathered in a dedicated hide or vessel, one is drawn among participants, and the result isolates an assigned share.
- Active motifs: وعاء يجمع سهام الميسر (`quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`); اقتراع يخرج السهم بين الشركاء (`quranic:root_001219:B004/m01`); إفراز النصيب وتقسيم الشيء (`quranic:root_001226:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ), 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ق س م`: 69:38 (أُقْسِمُ)
- Synthesis: Vessel, lots, draw, and apportioned result close one allocation scene. It materializes the visibly divided outcomes of 69:19 and 69:25, while usefully pressuring that primary reading by contrast: the lexical practice uses chance, but the Surah presents the handed result as certain judgment rather than a lucky draw.

### S2. Tipcat bat, strike, and audible impact
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A wooden bat strikes the qilla and produces both a physical mark and a heard impact.
- Active motifs: خشبة القال التي تضرب بها القلة (`quranic:root_001272:B008/m01`); قرع شيء وضربه بآلة (`quranic:root_001219:B001/m01`); أثر الضرب وصوت الوقوع (`quranic:root_001675:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ق و ل`: 69:19 (يَقُولُ), 69:25 (يَقُولُ), 69:40 (قَوْلُ), 69:41 (قَوْلِ), 69:42 (قَوْلِ), 69:44 (تَقَوَّلَ/أَقَاوِيلِ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)
- Synthesis: Bat, target, blow, impact trace, and sound form a complete game-stroke. The scene reframes the striking calamity of 69:4 through a controlled miniature collision: the same strike schema can be bounded play, whereas the Surah enlarges it into an event no player can contain or reverse.

### S3. Adhan, listening, standing, and response
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A caller announces prayer from an audible place; a hearer listens and accepts, stands to pray, answers the summons, and seals supplication with amen.
- Active motifs: الأذان والمؤذن وموضع بلوغ النداء (`quranic:root_000022:B003/m02`); الإصغاء وقبول المسموع (`quranic:root_000022:B002/m01`); القيام في الصلاة أو الذكر (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m02`); الصلاة المفروضة بقيامها وركوعها وسجودها (`quranic:root_000880:B001/m01`); جواب الداعي والتلبية (`quranic:root_001573:B003/m01`); قول آمين طلبا للاستجابة (`quranic:root_000054:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء ذ ن`: 69:12 (أُذُنٌ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ص ل ي`: 69:31 (صَلُّ); `ه ا ء`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ء م ن`: 69:33 (يُؤْمِنُ), 69:41 (تُؤْمِنُ)
- Synthesis: Caller, audible range, receptive hearer, standing body, prayer, answering voice, and amen close a congregational rite. It supports 69:12 by making the remembering ear an obedient response mechanism, and it reframes the glorification of 69:52 as an embodied communal answer rather than reception that stops at hearing.

### S4. Gestation, labor opening, and midwife reception
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A fetus is gathered and carried internally until labor approaches, the lower back opens, and a midwife receives the emerging child.
- Active motifs: الحمل في بطن المرأة أو الناقة (`quranic:root_000357:B002/m01`); قرب النتاج (`quranic:root_000493:B004/m01`); رحم يجمع دما أو جنينا (`quranic:root_001210:B004/m01`); انفراج الصلا عند الولادة ووقوع الولد (`quranic:root_000880:B006/m02`); القابلة تتلقى الولد عند الولادة (`quranic:root_001198:B007/m02`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `د ن و`: 69:23 (دَانِيَةٌ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ص ل ي`: 69:31 (صَلُّ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ)
- Synthesis: Interior gathering, gestation, approaching labor, bodily opening, emergence, and receiving hands form a complete birth scene. It materializes the Surah's movement from what is concealed at 69:18 to an outcome placed in the hand at 69:19 or 69:25: what develops unseen becomes unavoidable and publicly received.

### S5. Washing, shrouding, right-side placement, and burial
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A body is washed, wrapped and placed in a grave on its right side, commended in prayer, and covered with earth.
- Active motifs: غسل البدن وإزالة الدرن (`quranic:root_001087:B001/m02`); الكفن والقبر ومواراة الميت (`quranic:root_000266:B009/m01`); توسيد الميت على يمينه (`quranic:root_001698:B007/m01`); الدعاء للغير بالرحمة والاستغفار (`quranic:root_000880:B002/m01`); إهالة التراب على الميت ودفنه (`quranic:root_000482:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `غ س ل`: 69:36 (غِسْلِينٍ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ي م ن`: 69:19 (يَمِينِ), 69:45 (يَمِينِ); `ص ل ي`: 69:31 (صَلُّ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً)
- Synthesis: Washing, shroud, grave, right-side placement, prayer, and heaped soil close a burial rite. It reframes the exposure of 69:18 and the failed intervention of 69:47: burial closes and covers a body through communal care, while final presentation reopens concealment and leaves the account beyond human shielding.

### S6. Metered poem as sung ensemble
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A poet composes divided meter, then a singer, answering partner, and stringed instrument turn the verbal craft into a performed piece.
- Active motifs: قريض موزون وصناعة شاعر (`quranic:root_000798:B007/m01`); تقطيع البيت إلى أجزاء العروض (`quranic:root_001240:B022/m01`); الغناء بالصوت والتطريب (`quranic:root_001110:B003/m01`); المراسل في الغناء (`quranic:root_000563:B008/m02`); العود المزهر الذي يضرب عليه (`quranic:root_001058:B010/m02`)
- Ayah anchors: `ش ع ر`: 69:41 (شَاعِرٍ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `غ ن ي`: 69:28 (أَغْنَىٰ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ), 69:40 (رَسُولٍ); `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ)
- Synthesis: Composition, segmented meter, lead voice, answering performer, and instrument form a complete human production chain. The ensemble sharpens the denial at 69:41: "not a poet's word" excludes not merely a title but an authored, measured, and performed system, so revelation's acoustic force cannot be reduced to that craft.

### S7. Slaughter, skinning, roasting, and eating
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A blade stills a slaughtered animal, the hide is removed, flesh is divided and roasted, and the result is eaten.
- Active motifs: السكين التي تسكن اضطراب المذبوح (`quranic:root_000726:B007/m01`); إبقاء السالخ لحما أو شحما في الإهاب (`quranic:root_001102:B012/m01`); الصرم والإبانة الحسية (`quranic:root_001240:B001/m01`); شوي اللحم بالنار (`quranic:root_000880:B004/m02`); تناول المطعوم (`quranic:root_000043:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `ص ل ي`: 69:31 (صَلُّ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ)
- Synthesis: Knife, skinning trace, division, roasting, and eating close a butchery-to-meal process. It usefully pressures 69:30-37: ordinary processing produces nourishment for a living community, but the punitive sequence immobilizes a body and culminates in contaminating non-food, placing the condemned on the processed rather than the nourished side.

### S8. Postpartum brazier, hot water, and washing station
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A postpartum woman sits over a brazier or heated pit for an ailment, with hot water and a prepared bathing place completing the treatment.
- Active motifs: جلوس النفساء على المجمر أو في حفيرة موقدة من داء (`quranic:root_000450:B006/m01`); الماء الحار والاغتسال به (`quranic:root_000001:B002/m01`); موضع الاغتسال وآلة التسخين (`quranic:root_000001:B013/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ)
- Synthesis: Patient, heat source, hot medium, and washing station close a specific therapeutic practice. It reframes 69:31-36 by inversion: fire, heated water, and washing ordinarily aim at recovery and cleansing, whereas the punishment field turns them into exposure without cure or purification.

### S9. Foster calf, protected udder, and timed milk yield
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A dam separated from her calf is induced to accept another young animal; access to the udder is controlled, milk is allowed to return, and repeated milking sustains the household supply.
- Active motifs: ناقة نحي ولدها وتعطف على حوار غيرها لتدر (`quranic:root_000436:B010/m01`); شاة قريبة العهد بالولادة ملازمة للبيت للبن (`quranic:root_000532:B009/m01`); الشمال المشدود على ضرع الشاة (`quranic:root_000819:B004/m01`); رجوع اللبن في الضرع بين الحلبتين (`quranic:root_001188:B006/m01`); اللبن والدر المتتابع (`quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `خ ل و`: 69:24 (خَالِيَةِ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ), 69:43 (رَّبِّ), 69:52 (رَبِّ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ), 69:40 (رَسُولٍ)
- Synthesis: Foster transfer, recent birth, protected access, replenishment interval, and repeated yield form a complete lactation practice. It materializes the failure in 69:34 to encourage feeding the poor: provision depends on substituted care, restraint, waiting, and collection, not on passive possession of an animal or resource.

### S10. Arrow nock, point, and defective flight
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An arrow is fitted to the string at its nock and supplied with a point; point shape, a broken nock, or absent feathers determines its working form and trajectory.
- Active motifs: فوق السهم ومشق رأسه وموضع الوتر (`quranic:root_001188:B009/m01`); السلوف الطويل من نصال السهام (`quranic:root_000733:B014/m01`); المعراض السهم الذي يمضي عرضا أو بلا ريش (`quranic:root_001001:B013/m02`); النصل القصير العريض أو السهم المقطوع (`quranic:root_001240:B014/m02`)
- Ayah anchors: `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ)
- Synthesis: Nock, string, point, feathers, and trajectory form a complete projectile mechanism. It reframes the alignment of saying, messenger, and source in 69:40-44, while making the precise cut of 69:45-46 concrete: a broken nock or unfeathered shaft travels awry, whereas fitted parts carry directed force.

## Cross-Pericope Channels

### X1. P1 -> P2 -> P3: Seizure narrows its target
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (69:1-18) -> P2 (69:19-37) -> P3 (69:38-52); repeated scene signature and role progression.
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: The same superior taking first overtakes a disobedient people, then arrests and collars one condemned person, and finally threatens a putative fabricating messenger with a precise severing of the lifeline.
- Active motifs: حوز الشيء وتناوله (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`); المؤاخذة بالذنب (`quranic:root_000018:B002/m01`); القبض والأسر (`quranic:root_000018:B003/m01`); طوق يغل العضو (`quranic:root_001102:B006/m01`); الصرم والإبانة (`quranic:root_001240:B001/m01`); الوتين عرق الحياة (`quranic:root_001622:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً), 69:30 (خُذُ), 69:45 (أَخَذْ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ق ط ع`: 69:46 (قَطَعْ); `و ت ن`: 69:46 (وَتِينَ)
- Synthesis: `ء خ ذ` is the only surface operation repeated in all three pericopes. Its target contracts from the historical collective in P1, to the judged individual in P2, to the hypothetical false carrier in P3; its endpoint likewise sharpens from overwhelming capture, to fixed restraint, to a cut at the life-artery. The progression joins historical judgment, eschatological sentence, and revelation-authentication under one rule of answerability.

### X2. P1 -> P2 -> P3: A heard warning becomes a readable account
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (69:1-18) -> P2 (69:19-37) -> P3 (69:38-52); causal sequence and role progression from warning, through record, to authenticated reminder.
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A messenger carries a warning to an ear that may accept and retain it; the receiver's response later appears as a written record read aloud, while the closing pericope identifies the saying's carrier, renews the reminder, and names continued denial.
- Active motifs: الرسول والرسالة (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); الإصغاء وقبول المسموع (`quranic:root_000022:B002/m01`); ضم المعنى في القلب (`quranic:root_001664:B001/m01`); نظم الحروف واسم المكتوب (`quranic:root_001283:B002/m01`); قراءة وتلاوة وإقراء (`quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`); الذكرى والتذكرة ما يذكّر (`quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`); خلاف الصدق (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ), 69:40 (رَسُولٍ); `ء ذ ن`: 69:12 (أُذُنٌ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ك ت ب`: 69:19 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِ), 69:25 (كِتَٰبَ/كِتَٰبِيَ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ذ ك ر`: 69:12 (تَذْكِرَةً), 69:42 (تَذَكَّرُ), 69:48 (تَذْكِرَةٌ); `ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ), 69:49 (مُّكَذِّبِينَ)
- Synthesis: P1 supplies the carrier, receptive organ, and retained reminder; P2 externalizes response as a personal text placed before readers; P3 certifies the carrier and source while dividing remembrance from denial. The bridge is causal rather than topical: hearing becomes accountable reception, reception becomes record, and the record's verdict preserves the original relation to the message.

### X3. P1 -> P2: Punishment changes from measured duration to measured reach
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (69:1-18) -> P2 (69:19-37); repeated scene signature and scale contrast.
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Seven marks the duration for which a destructive environment encloses a people, while seventy cubits marks the spatial reach of the chain that encloses one body.
- Active motifs: العدد سبعة وما يتفرع منه (`quranic:root_000669:B001/m01`); القياس بالذراع (`quranic:root_000512:B002/m01`); الذرع طاقة ومدى (`quranic:root_000512:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `س ب ع`: 69:7 (سَبْعَ), 69:32 (سَبْعُونَ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا)
- Synthesis: The repeated numerical stem converts temporal exposure in P1 into spatial restraint in P2. Seven nights exceed communal endurance; seventy body-derived units exceed individual reach and capacity. Measurement therefore performs the same scene twice at different scales, giving historical storm and eschatological chain a common boundary of inescapability.

### X4. P1 -> P2 -> P3: Inquiry becomes wished ignorance and then certainty
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (69:1-18) -> P2 (69:19-37) -> P3 (69:38-52); contrast and epistemic role progression.
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: The opening asks what could make the hearer know the binding reality; the right-hand recipient anticipates the account from signs, the left-hand recipient wishes not to know it, and the close resolves the movement as fixed truth and certainty.
- Active motifs: الدراية والعلم (`quranic:root_000473:B001/m01`); يقين بعد أمارة (`quranic:root_000969:B001/m01`); العد والحساب (`quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`); ثبات مطابق للواقع ضد الباطل (`quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`); ثبات العلم وزوال الشك (`quranic:root_001696:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `د ر ي`: 69:3 (أَدْرَىٰ), 69:26 (أَدْرِ); `ظ ن ن`: 69:20 (ظَنَن); `ح س ب`: 69:20 (حِسَابِ), 69:26 (حِسَابِ); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:51 (حَقُّ); `ي ق ن`: 69:51 (يَقِينِ)
- Synthesis: The repeated `د ر ي` reverses the knower's stance: P1 opens an epistemic gap, whereas P2 makes the condemned wish that gap had never closed. The vindicated person's sign-based conviction supplies the positive middle path, and P3 joins the opening `ح ق ق` frame to `ي ق ن`, ending inquiry in knowledge that can no longer be deferred or refused.

### X5. P1 -> P2 -> P3: Effective carriage gives way to failed human protection
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (69:1-18) -> P2 (69:19-37) -> P3 (69:38-52); contrast and role progression in protection.
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: P1 preserves a vulnerable collective by carrying it through the flood; P2 removes the intimate person who might remain near the condemned; P3 denies that anyone can interpose as a barrier against enforcement.
- Active motifs: إقلال المحمول الظاهر (`quranic:root_000357:B001/m01`); الحميم والحامة في القربى (`quranic:root_000001:B011/m01`); الحيلولة بفاصل (`quranic:root_000297:B001/m01`); استغراق النفي (`quranic:root_000017:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `ح ج ز`: 69:47 (حَٰجِزِينَ); `ء ح د`: 69:47 (أَحَدٍ)
- Synthesis: Protection changes role across the Surah: authorized carriage succeeds in P1, ordinary social proximity is absent in P2, and attempted intervention is universally negated in P3. The contrast distinguishes preservation granted by the governing agent from kinship or obstruction imagined as independent shields, progressively stripping the condemned of substitute protection.
